## Comprehensive Codebase Exploration Report

I've thoroughly explored the DuckDB Data Agent codebase. Here are my findings:

### 1. **Overall Project Structure**

The project is a hybrid SQL playground + AI-powered agent:
- **Frontend**: React 18 + TypeScript with Vite, located in `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/frontend/src/`
- **Backend**: FastAPI + DuckDB + Claude Agent SDK, located in `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/`

Key files:
- Backend: `main.py`, `database.py`, `agent.py`, `tools.py`, `routes/` (tables.py, query.py, chat.py)
- Frontend: `App.tsx`, `AgentContext.tsx`, `AgentPanel.tsx`, `MessageBubble.tsx`, `components/`, `agent/agentService.ts`

---

### 2. **How Agent Mode Works**

**Backend Flow (`/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/agent.py`):**

1. **`stream_chat()` function** receives a user message and sets up the Claude Agent SDK
2. **`ClaudeAgentOptions` configuration**:
   - `system_prompt`: Built dynamically with table schema info
   - `mcp_servers`: Registers DuckDB server with `create_duckdb_server()`
   - `allowed_tools`: Only allows `"mcp__duckdb__execute_sql"`
   - `permission_mode`: "bypassPermissions" for tool execution
   - `include_partial_messages`: True for streaming

3. **Message streaming loop**: Processes events from `client._query.receive_messages()`:
   - Parses messages using `parse_message()` from Claude SDK
   - Handles three message types: `StreamEvent`, `AssistantMessage`, `ResultMessage`

4. **Event processing**:
   - `content_block_delta` with `text_delta`: Thinking text updates → sends SSE `"thinking"` or `"answer"` events
   - `content_block_start` with `tool_use` type: Signals tool call starting
   - `AssistantMessage` with `ToolUseBlock`: Executes SQL immediately and sends results back to SDK
   - `ResultMessage`: Signals completion

5. **Tool execution** (`/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/tools.py`):
   - `@tool` decorator defines `execute_sql` MCP tool
   - Takes `sql` argument, executes via `db.execute_query()`
   - Returns structured JSON with columns, rows, rowCount
   - Handles errors gracefully with `"is_error": True` flag

**Frontend Flow (`/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/frontend/src/`):**

1. **`AgentContext.tsx`**: React context managing agent state
   - `sendMessage()`: Initiates agent loop via `runAgentLoop()`
   - Manages segments (`ContentSegment[]`) separating thinking/tool/answer content
   - Buffers text with 50ms flush timer for UI performance

2. **`agentService.ts`**: SSE streaming parser
   - Connects to `/api/chat` endpoint
   - Handles events: `"thinking"`, `"answer"`, `"tool_call"`, `"tool_result"`, `"done"`, `"error"`
   - Invokes callbacks for each event type

3. **Callbacks in `AgentContext.tsx`**:
   - `onTextChunk`: Buffers text for thinking/answer phases
   - `onToolCall`: Creates pending tool segment
   - `onToolResult`: Replaces pending segment with full results
   - `onDone`: Marks answer segment, stops streaming

---

### 3. **Thinking Block Rendering**

**Location**: `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/frontend/src/components/MessageBubble.tsx`

**`ThinkingBlock` component** (lines 25-72):
- Filters segments where `type !== 'answer'` (includes thinking + tool segments)
- Uses HTML `<details>` element with `open={isActivelyStreaming}` for collapse/expand
- Renders thinking content with ReactMarkdown
- Renders inline query results inside thinking block
- Shows preview of last thinking line in collapsed state

**Key features**:
- `getLastThinkingLine()` extracts last non-empty line from thinking for summary
- Uses CSS classes: `.message-bubble__collapsible`, `.message-bubble__thinking-body`
- Interactive expand/collapse with custom arrow icon
- Streaming remainder text displayed while still processing

**CSS styling** (`/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/frontend/src/components/MessageBubble.css`):
- Gray background with left border for thinking block
- Distinct "Answer" label for answer segments with green styling

---

### 4. **Code Accessing `.description` Attribute**

**Single location**: `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/database.py` line 14

```python
def execute_query(self, sql: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    result = self.conn.execute(sql)
    columns = [desc[0] for desc in result.description] if result.description else []
    rows = [dict(zip(columns, row)) for row in result.fetchall()] if columns else []
    return {"columns": columns, "rows": rows, "rowCount": len(rows)}
```

**Analysis**:
- `result` is a DuckDB cursor object from `duckdb.connect().execute()`
- `.description` contains tuple of column metadata (name, type, etc.)
- **Safe**: Checks `if result.description` before accessing
- **DuckDB guarantee**: Always has `.description` (tested with CREATE, INSERT, DROP, EXPLAIN, SELECT)
- No `None` risk here - this is defensive programming

**Where it could fail** (theoretical):
- If `result` itself is `None` (but `execute()` always returns a cursor)
- If a custom database wrapper replaces `.description` with `None`

---

### 5. **SQL vs Non-SQL Query Handling**

**Router differences** (`/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/routes/`):

**`/api/query` (query.py - SQL Playground)**:
```python
@router.post("/query")
async def execute_query(request: QueryRequest):
    result = db.execute_query(request.sql)
    sql_lower = request.sql.strip().lower()
    result_type = "markdown" if sql_lower.startswith("explain") else "table"
    return {**result, "resultType": result_type}
```

**`/api/chat` (chat.py - Agent Mode)**:
- Returns `StreamingResponse` with SSE events
- Agent parses `content_block_delta` for thinking text
- Uses streaming for real-time token display

**Key difference**:
- **Non-agent queries**: Direct execution, returns full result with `resultType` ("table" or "markdown")
- **Agent queries**: Streaming via SSE, Claude generates reasoning + SQL, SDK handles tool execution
- **EXPLAIN handling**: Both detect `EXPLAIN` prefix and return as markdown instead of table

**Frontend rendering** (`/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/frontend/src/App.tsx`):
```typescript
{queryResult?.resultType === 'markdown' ? (
  <ResultMarkdown result={queryResult} />
) : (
  <ResultsTable result={queryResult} />
)}
```

---

### 6. **Additional Key Findings**

**Types** (`/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/frontend/src/types.ts`):
- `ContentSegment`: `{ type: 'thinking' | 'tool' | 'answer', text?, toolResult? }`
- `ToolCallResult`: Contains `toolCallId`, `sql`, `columns`, `rows`, `rowCount`, `error?`, `rawContent?`
- `ChatMessage`: Has `segments?` array for segmented mode

**Routes Summary**:
- `GET /api/health`: Health check
- `GET /api/tables`: List all tables
- `POST /api/upload`: Upload CSV file
- `POST /api/upload/sample`: Load Titanic dataset
- `DELETE /api/tables/{name}`: Drop table
- `POST /api/query`: Execute SQL (non-agent)
- `POST /api/chat`: Stream agent responses (SSE)

**Dependencies** (`/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/pyproject.toml`):
- `fastapi`, `uvicorn`, `duckdb`, `python-dotenv`, `python-multipart`
- **`claude-agent-sdk ^0.1.38`**: Core agent functionality

---

This codebase demonstrates a well-structured separation between the traditional SQL playground (direct execution + table results) and the AI agent (streaming thinking blocks with inline tool results). The thinking block rendering elegantly shows the agent's reasoning process with expandable details elements.

     2→import logging
     3→from typing import AsyncIterator
     4→
     5→from claude_agent_sdk import (
     6→    ClaudeSDKClient,

    12→from claude_agent_sdk._errors import MessageParseError
    13→from claude_agent_sdk._internal.message_parser import parse_message
    14→from claude_agent_sdk.types import StreamEvent
    15→from app.tools import create_duckdb_server
    16→from app.database import db
    17→
    18→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    19→
    20→
    21→def build_system_prompt() -> str:
    22→    tables = db.list_tables()
    23→    prompt = """You are a helpful data analyst assistant working with a DuckDB database.
    24→You can execute SQL queries using the execute_sql tool to answer questions about the user's data.
    25→
    26→Guidelines:
    27→- Write clear, efficient DuckDB SQL queries
    28→- When exploring data, start with small queries (use LIMIT)
    29→- Explain your findings in plain language after getting results
    30→- If a query fails, try to fix it and retry
    31→- Use double quotes for table and column names that might conflict with reserved words
    32→"""
    33→    if not tables:
    34→        prompt += "\nNo tables are currently loaded. Ask the user to upload a CSV file first."
    35→    else:
    36→        prompt += "\nCurrently loaded tables:\n"
    37→        for table in tables:
    38→            prompt += f'\nTable: "{table["name"]}" ({table["rowCount"]} rows)\nColumns:\n'
    39→            for col in table["columns"]:
    40→                prompt += f'  - "{col["name"]}" ({col["type"]})\n'
    41→    return prompt
    42→
    43→
    44→async def stream_chat(message: str, session_id: str | None = None) -> AsyncIterator[str]:
    45→    """Stream agent chat responses as SSE events."""
    46→    duckdb_server = create_duckdb_server()
    47→
    48→    options = ClaudeAgentOptions(
    49→        system_prompt=build_system_prompt(),
    50→        mcp_servers={"duckdb": duckdb_server},
    51→        allowed_tools=["mcp__duckdb__execute_sql"],
    52→        permission_mode="bypassPermissions",
    53→        max_turns=20,
    54→        include_partial_messages=True,
    55→    )
    56→
    57→    client = ClaudeSDKClient(options=options)
    58→    actual_session_id = session_id
    59→
    60→    try:
    61→        await client.connect()
    62→        await client.query(message, session_id=session_id or "default")
    63→
    64→        current_text = ""
    65→        has_tool_calls = False
    66→        thinking_sent = False
    67→
    68→        async for raw_data in client._query.receive_messages():
    69→            try:
    70→                msg = parse_message(raw_data)
    71→            except MessageParseError as e:
    72→                logger.debug("Skipping unrecognized message: %s", e)
    73→                continue
    74→
    75→            if isinstance(msg, StreamEvent):
    76→                event = msg.event
    77→                if not actual_session_id:
    78→                    actual_session_id = msg.session_id
    79→
    80→                event_type = event.get("type", "")
    81→
    82→                if event_type == "content_block_delta":
    83→                    delta = event.get("delta", {})
    84→                    delta_type = delta.get("type", "")
    85→                    if delta_type == "text_delta":
    86→                        text = delta.get("text", "")
    87→                        current_text += text
    88→                        event_name = "thinking" if not has_tool_calls else "answer"
    89→                        yield f"event: {event_name}\ndata: {json.dumps({'text': text})}\n\n"
    90→
    91→                elif event_type == "content_block_start":
    92→                    block = event.get("content_block", {})
    93→                    if block.get("type") == "tool_use":
    94→                        if current_text.strip() and not thinking_sent:
    95→                            thinking_sent = True
    96→                        has_tool_calls = True
    97→
    98→            elif isinstance(msg, AssistantMessage):
    99→                if not actual_session_id:
   100→                    actual_session_id = "default"
   101→
   102→                for block in msg.content:
   103→                    if isinstance(block, ToolUseBlock):
   104→                        sql = block.input.get("sql", "")
   105→                        yield f"event: tool_call\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': block.id, 'sql': sql})}\n\n"
   106→                        has_tool_calls = True
   107→
   108→                        # Execute the query directly for the UI since
   109→                        # ToolResultBlock is not exposed in the SDK stream
   110→                        try:
   111→                            result = db.execute_query(sql)
   112→                            truncated = result["rows"][:100]
   113→                            yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': block.id, 'sql': sql, 'columns': result['columns'], 'rows': truncated, 'rowCount': result['rowCount']}, default=str)}\n\n"
   114→                        except Exception as e:
   115→                            yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': block.id, 'sql': sql, 'error': str(e)})}\n\n"
   116→
   117→            elif isinstance(msg, ResultMessage):
   118→                actual_session_id = msg.session_id
   119→                if msg.is_error and msg.result:
   120→                    yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': msg.result})}\n\n"
   121→                yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
   122→                break
   123→
   124→    except Exception as e:
   125→        yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': str(e)})}\n\n"
   126→    finally:
   127→        try:
   128→            await client.disconnect()
   129→        except Exception:
   130→            pass
   131→

     1→import duckdb
     2→import os
     3→import tempfile
     4→from typing import Any
     5→
     6→
     7→class Database:
     8→    def __init__(self):
     9→        self.conn = duckdb.connect(":memory:")
    10→
    11→    def execute_query(self, sql: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    12→        """Execute a SQL query and return results as dict with columns, rows, rowCount."""
    13→        result = self.conn.execute(sql)
    14→        columns = [desc[0] for desc in result.description] if result.description else []
    15→        rows = [dict(zip(columns, row)) for row in result.fetchall()] if columns else []
    16→        return {"columns": columns, "rows": rows, "rowCount": len(rows)}
    17→
    18→    def load_csv(self, file_bytes: bytes, filename: str, table_name: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    19→        """Load a CSV file into a DuckDB table. Returns table info."""
    20→        with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(suffix=".csv", delete=False) as tmp:
    21→            tmp.write(file_bytes)
    22→            tmp_path = tmp.name
    23→        try:
    24→            self.conn.execute(
    25→                f'CREATE OR REPLACE TABLE "{table_name}" AS SELECT * FROM read_csv_auto(\'{tmp_path}\')'
    26→            )
    27→        finally:
    28→            os.unlink(tmp_path)
    29→        return self.get_table_info(table_name)
    30→
    31→    def get_table_info(self, table_name: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    32→        """Get info about a specific table."""
    33→        cols_result = self.conn.execute(f'DESCRIBE "{table_name}"')
    34→        columns = [
    35→            {"name": row[0], "type": row[1]}
    36→            for row in cols_result.fetchall()
    37→        ]
    38→        count_result = self.conn.execute(f'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM "{table_name}"')
    39→        row_count = count_result.fetchone()[0]
    40→        return {"name": table_name, "columns": columns, "rowCount": row_count}
    41→
    42→    def list_tables(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    43→        """List all tables with their schema info."""
    44→        tables_result = self.conn.execute("SHOW TABLES")
    45→        table_names = [row[0] for row in tables_result.fetchall()]
    46→        return [self.get_table_info(name) for name in table_names]
    47→
    48→    def drop_table(self, table_name: str) -> None:
    49→        """Drop a table."""
    50→        self.conn.execute(f'DROP TABLE IF EXISTS "{table_name}"')
    51→
    52→    def load_sample_data(self, csv_path: str, table_name: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    53→        """Load sample CSV from a file path."""
    54→        self.conn.execute(
    55→            f'CREATE OR REPLACE TABLE "{table_name}" AS SELECT * FROM read_csv_auto(\'{csv_path}\')'
    56→        )
    57→        return self.get_table_info(table_name)
    58→
    59→
    60→# Singleton instance
    61→db = Database()
    62→

     1→import json
     2→from typing import Any
     3→from claude_agent_sdk import tool, create_sdk_mcp_server
     4→from app.database import db
     5→
     6→MAX_RESULT_ROWS = 100
     7→
     8→
     9→@tool("execute_sql", "Execute a SQL query against the DuckDB database. Use this to query loaded tables, create views, or run any valid DuckDB SQL. Results are returned as JSON with columns, rows, and rowCount.", {"sql": str})
    10→async def execute_sql(args: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    11→    sql = args["sql"]
    12→    try:
    13→        result = db.execute_query(sql)
    14→        truncated_rows = result["rows"][:MAX_RESULT_ROWS]
    15→        result_json = {
    16→            "status": "success",
    17→            "columns": result["columns"],
    18→            "rows": truncated_rows,
    19→            "rowCount": result["rowCount"],
    20→        }
    21→        content_text = json.dumps(result_json, default=str)
    22→        return {"content": [{"type": "text", "text": content_text}]}
    23→    except Exception as e:
    24→        error_json = {"status": "error", "error": str(e)}
    25→        return {
    26→            "content": [{"type": "text", "text": json.dumps(error_json)}],
    27→            "is_error": True,
    28→        }
    29→
    30→
    31→def create_duckdb_server():
    32→    return create_sdk_mcp_server(
    33→        name="duckdb",
    34→        version="1.0.0",
    35→        tools=[execute_sql],
    36→    )
    37→
```

     1→import ReactMarkdown from 'react-markdown';
     2→import remarkGfm from 'remark-gfm';
     3→import type { ChatMessage, ContentSegment } from '../types';
     4→import { InlineQueryResult } from './InlineQueryResult';
     5→import './MessageBubble.css';
     6→
     7→function getLastThinkingLine(segments: ContentSegment[], streamingRemainder?: string): string {
     8→  // Use streaming remainder if available
     9→  if (streamingRemainder?.trim()) {
    10→    const lines = streamingRemainder.trim().split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim());
    11→    const last = lines[lines.length - 1] || '';
    12→    return last.length > 100 ? last.slice(0, 100) + '...' : last;
    13→  }
    14→  // Otherwise use last thinking segment's last line
    15→  for (let i = segments.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    16→    if (segments[i].type === 'thinking' && segments[i].text?.trim()) {
    17→      const lines = segments[i].text!.trim().split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim());
    18→      const last = lines[lines.length - 1] || '';
    19→      return last.length > 100 ? last.slice(0, 100) + '...' : last;
    20→    }
    21→  }
    22→  return 'Thinking...';
    23→}
    24→
    25→function ThinkingBlock({ segments, streamingRemainder, isActivelyStreaming }: {
    26→  segments: ContentSegment[];
    27→  streamingRemainder?: string;
    28→  isActivelyStreaming: boolean;
    29→}) {
    30→  // All non-answer segments go inside the thinking block
    31→  const thinkingSegments = segments.filter((s) => s.type !== 'answer');
    32→  const hasContent = thinkingSegments.some(
    33→    (s) => (s.type === 'thinking' && s.text?.trim()) || (s.type === 'tool' && s.toolResult)
    34→  ) || streamingRemainder?.trim();
    35→
    36→  if (!hasContent) return null;
    37→
    38→  const summary = getLastThinkingLine(segments, streamingRemainder);
    39→
    40→  return (
    41→    <details className="message-bubble__segment message-bubble__segment--thinking message-bubble__collapsible" open={isActivelyStreaming || undefined}>
    42→      <summary className="message-bubble__collapsible-summary">
    43→        <span className="message-bubble__segment-label">Thinking</span>
    44→        <span className="message-bubble__collapsible-preview">{summary}</span>
    45→      </summary>
    46→      <div className="message-bubble__thinking-body">
    47→        {thinkingSegments.map((seg, i) => {
    48→          if (seg.type === 'thinking' && seg.text?.trim()) {
    49→            return (
    50→              <div key={i} className="message-bubble__segment-content">
    51→                <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}>{seg.text}</ReactMarkdown>
    52→              </div>
    53→            );
    54→          }
    55→          if (seg.type === 'tool' && seg.toolResult) {
    56→            return (
    57→              <div key={i} className="message-bubble__tool-segment">
    58→                <InlineQueryResult result={seg.toolResult} />
    59→              </div>
    60→            );
    61→          }
    62→          return null;
    63→        })}
    64→        {streamingRemainder?.trim() && (
    65→          <div className="message-bubble__segment-content">
    66→            <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}>{streamingRemainder}</ReactMarkdown>
    67→          </div>
    68→        )}
    69→      </div>
    70→    </details>
    71→  );
    72→}
    73→
    74→export function MessageBubble({ message }: { message: ChatMessage }) {
    75→  const isUser = message.role === 'user';
    76→  const hasSegments = !isUser && message.segments && message.segments.length > 0;
    77→
    78→  // Compute streaming remainder text
    79→  let streamingRemainder: string | undefined;
    80→  if (hasSegments && message.isStreaming && message.content) {
    81→    const segmentedText = message.segments!
    82→      .filter((s) => s.type !== 'tool')
    83→      .map((s) => s.text || '')
    84→      .join('');
    85→    const remaining = message.content.slice(segmentedText.length);
    86→    if (remaining.trim()) {
    87→      streamingRemainder = remaining;
    88→    }
    89→  }
    90→
    91→  // Check if currently in a thinking-only phase (no answer segment yet)
    92→  const hasAnswer = hasSegments && message.segments!.some((s) => s.type === 'answer');
    93→  const isThinkingPhase = !!message.isStreaming && !hasAnswer;
    94→
    95→  // Extract answer segments
    96→  const answerSegments = hasSegments
    97→    ? message.segments!.filter((s) => s.type === 'answer' && s.text?.trim())
    98→    : [];
    99→
   100→  return (
   101→    <div className={`message-bubble message-bubble--${message.role}`}>
   102→      <div className="message-bubble__header">
   103→        {isUser ? 'You' : 'Assistant'}
   104→      </div>
   105→
   106→      {hasSegments ? (
   107→        <div className="message-bubble__segments">
   108→          <ThinkingBlock
   109→            segments={message.segments!}
   110→            streamingRemainder={isThinkingPhase ? streamingRemainder : undefined}
   111→            isActivelyStreaming={isThinkingPhase}
   112→          />
   113→          {answerSegments.map((seg, i) => (
   114→            <div key={i} className="message-bubble__segment message-bubble__segment--answer">
   115→              <div className="message-bubble__segment-label message-bubble__segment-label--answer">Answer</div>
   116→              <div className="message-bubble__segment-content">
   117→                <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}>{seg.text!}</ReactMarkdown>
   118→              </div>
   119→            </div>
   120→          ))}
   121→          {message.isStreaming && !message.content && (
   122→            <span className="message-bubble__typing">Thinking...</span>
   123→          )}
   124→        </div>
   125→      ) : (
   126→        <>
   127→          <div className="message-bubble__content">
   128→            {message.content ? (
   129→              <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}>{message.content}</ReactMarkdown>
   130→            ) : message.isStreaming ? (
   131→              <span className="message-bubble__typing">Thinking...</span>
   132→            ) : null}
   133→          </div>
   134→          {message.toolCalls && message.toolCalls.length > 0 && (
   135→            <div className="message-bubble__tools">
   136→              {message.toolCalls.map((tc) => (
   137→                <InlineQueryResult key={tc.toolCallId} result={tc} />
   138→              ))}
   139→            </div>
   140→          )}
   141→        </>
   142→      )}
   143→    </div>
   144→  );
   145→}
   146→
```

     1→import {
     2→  createContext,
     3→  useCallback,
     4→  useContext,
     5→  useRef,
     6→  useState,
     7→  type ReactNode,
     8→} from 'react';
     9→import { runAgentLoop } from './agent/agentService';
    10→import type { ChatMessage, ContentSegment, TableInfo, ToolCallResult } from './types';
    11→
    12→interface AgentContextValue {
    13→  messages: ChatMessage[];
    14→  isStreaming: boolean;
    15→  sendMessage: (text: string) => void;
    16→  clearMessages: () => void;
    17→}
    18→
    19→const AgentContext = createContext<AgentContextValue>({
    20→  messages: [],
    21→  isStreaming: false,
    22→  sendMessage: () => {},
    23→  clearMessages: () => {},
    24→});
    25→
    26→function generateId() {
    27→  return Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10);
    28→}
    29→
    30→export function AgentProvider({
    31→  children,
    32→  refreshTables,
    33→}: {
    34→  children: ReactNode;
    35→  tables: TableInfo[];
    36→  refreshTables: () => Promise<void>;
    37→}) {
    38→  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<ChatMessage[]>([]);
    39→  const [isStreaming, setIsStreaming] = useState(false);
    40→  const abortRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);
    41→  const textBufferRef = useRef('');
    42→  const flushTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
    43→  const assistantIdRef = useRef('');
    44→  const segmentsRef = useRef<ContentSegment[]>([]);
    45→  const currentTextRef = useRef('');
    46→  const sessionIdRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
    47→
    48→  const flushText = useCallback(() => {
    49→    const text = textBufferRef.current;
    50→    if (!text) return;
    51→    const id = assistantIdRef.current;
    52→    currentTextRef.current += text;
    53→    setMessages((prev) =>
    54→      prev.map((m) =>
    55→        m.id === id ? { ...m, content: m.content + text } : m
    56→      )
    57→    );
    58→    textBufferRef.current = '';
    59→  }, []);
    60→
    61→  const sendMessage = useCallback(
    62→    async (text: string) => {
    63→      if (isStreaming) return;
    64→
    65→      const userMsg: ChatMessage = {
    66→        id: generateId(),
    67→        role: 'user',
    68→        content: text,
    69→      };
    70→
    71→      const assistantId = generateId();
    72→      assistantIdRef.current = assistantId;
    73→      const assistantMsg: ChatMessage = {
    74→        id: assistantId,
    75→        role: 'assistant',
    76→        content: '',
    77→        toolCalls: [],
    78→        isStreaming: true,
    79→      };
    80→
    81→      setMessages((prev) => [...prev, userMsg, assistantMsg]);
    82→      setIsStreaming(true);
    83→      textBufferRef.current = '';
    84→      segmentsRef.current = [];
    85→      currentTextRef.current = '';
    86→
    87→      const controller = new AbortController();
    88→      abortRef.current = controller;
    89→
    90→      await runAgentLoop(
    91→        text,
    92→        sessionIdRef.current,
    93→        {
    94→          onTextChunk: (chunk) => {
    95→            textBufferRef.current += chunk;
    96→            if (!flushTimerRef.current) {
    97→              flushTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
    98→                flushText();
    99→                flushTimerRef.current = null;
   100→              }, 50);
   101→            }
   102→          },
   103→          onToolCall: (toolCallId: string, sql: string) => {
   104→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   105→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   106→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   107→            }
   108→            flushText();
   109→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   110→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'thinking', text: currentTextRef.current });
   111→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   112→            }
   113→            // Add a pending tool segment so the SQL is shown immediately
   114→            segmentsRef.current.push({
   115→              type: 'tool',
   116→              toolResult: { toolCallId, sql, columns: [], rows: [], rowCount: 0 },
   117→            });
   118→            setMessages((prev) =>
   119→              prev.map((m) =>
   120→                m.id === assistantId
   121→                  ? { ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   122→                  : m
   123→              )
   124→            );
   125→          },
   126→          onToolResult: (result: ToolCallResult) => {
   127→            // Replace the pending tool segment with the full result
   128→            const pendingIdx = segmentsRef.current.findIndex(
   129→              (s) => s.type === 'tool' && s.toolResult?.toolCallId === result.toolCallId
   130→            );
   131→            if (pendingIdx !== -1) {
   132→              segmentsRef.current[pendingIdx] = { type: 'tool', toolResult: result };
   133→            } else {
   134→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'tool', toolResult: result });
   135→            }
   136→            setMessages((prev) =>
   137→              prev.map((m) =>
   138→                m.id === assistantId
   139→                  ? { ...m, toolCalls: [...(m.toolCalls || []), result], segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   140→                  : m
   141→              )
   142→            );
   143→            refreshTables();
   144→          },
   145→          onDone: (newSessionId) => {
   146→            if (newSessionId) sessionIdRef.current = newSessionId;
   147→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   148→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   149→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   150→            }
   151→            flushText();
   152→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   153→              segmentsRef.current.push({
   154→                type: 'answer',
   155→                text: currentTextRef.current,
   156→              });
   157→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   158→            }
   159→            setMessages((prev) =>
   160→              prev.map((m) =>
   161→                m.id === assistantId
   162→                  ? { ...m, isStreaming: false, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   163→                  : m
   164→              )
   165→            );
   166→            setIsStreaming(false);
   167→            abortRef.current = null;
   168→          },
   169→          onError: (error) => {
   170→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   171→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   172→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   173→            }
   174→            flushText();
   175→            setMessages((prev) =>
   176→              prev.map((m) =>
   177→                m.id === assistantId
   178→                  ? { ...m, content: m.content + `\n\n**Error:** ${error}`, isStreaming: false }
   179→                  : m
   180→              )
   181→            );
   182→            setIsStreaming(false);
   183→            abortRef.current = null;
   184→          },
   185→        },
   186→        controller.signal
   187→      );
   188→    },
   189→    [isStreaming, messages, flushText, refreshTables]
   190→  );
   191→
   192→  const clearMessages = useCallback(() => {
   193→    if (abortRef.current) {
   194→      abortRef.current.abort();
   195→    }
   196→    setMessages([]);
   197→    setIsStreaming(false);
   198→    sessionIdRef.current = null;
   199→  }, []);
   200→
   201→  return (
   202→    <AgentContext.Provider
   203→      value={{ messages, isStreaming, sendMessage, clearMessages }}
   204→    >
   205→      {children}
   206→    </AgentContext.Provider>
   207→  );
   208→}
   209→
   210→export function useAgent() {
   211→  return useContext(AgentContext);
   212→}
   213→

     1→import type { ToolCallResult } from '../types';
     2→
     3→interface AgentCallbacks {
     4→  onTextChunk: (text: string) => void;
     5→  onToolCall: (toolCallId: string, sql: string) => void;
     6→  onToolResult: (result: ToolCallResult) => void;
     7→  onDone: (sessionId: string | null) => void;
     8→  onError: (error: string) => void;
     9→}
    10→
    11→export async function runAgentLoop(
    12→  message: string,
    13→  sessionId: string | null,
    14→  callbacks: AgentCallbacks,
    15→  signal?: AbortSignal,
    16→): Promise<void> {
    17→  try {
    18→    const response = await fetch('/api/chat', {
    19→      method: 'POST',
    20→      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    21→      body: JSON.stringify({ message, session_id: sessionId }),
    22→      signal,
    23→    });
    24→
    25→    if (!response.ok) {
    26→      const errorText = await response.text();
    27→      callbacks.onError(`Server error: ${errorText}`);
    28→      return;
    29→    }
    30→
    31→    const reader = response.body?.getReader();
    32→    if (!reader) {
    33→      callbacks.onError('No response stream');
    34→      return;
    35→    }
    36→
    37→    const decoder = new TextDecoder();
    38→    let buffer = '';
    39→
    40→    while (true) {
    41→      const { done, value } = await reader.read();
    42→      if (done) break;
    43→
    44→      buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
    45→      const lines = buffer.split('\n');
    46→      buffer = lines.pop() ?? '';
    47→
    48→      let eventType = '';
    49→      for (const line of lines) {
    50→        if (line.startsWith('event: ')) {
    51→          eventType = line.slice(7).trim();
    52→        } else if (line.startsWith('data: ')) {
    53→          const data = line.slice(6);
    54→          try {
    55→            const parsed = JSON.parse(data);
    56→            handleSSEEvent(eventType, parsed, callbacks);
    57→          } catch {
    58→            // Skip malformed JSON
    59→          }
    60→          eventType = '';
    61→        }
    62→      }
    63→    }
    64→  } catch (e: unknown) {
    65→    if (signal?.aborted) return;
    66→    const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Connection failed';
    67→    callbacks.onError(msg);
    68→  }
    69→}
    70→
    71→function handleSSEEvent(
    72→  eventType: string,
    73→  data: Record<string, unknown>,
    74→  callbacks: AgentCallbacks,
    75→) {
    76→  switch (eventType) {
    77→    case 'thinking':
    78→    case 'answer':
    79→      callbacks.onTextChunk(data.text as string);
    80→      break;
    81→    case 'tool_call':
    82→      callbacks.onToolCall(data.id as string, data.sql as string);
    83→      break;
    84→    case 'tool_result': {
    85→      const result: ToolCallResult = {
    86→        toolCallId: (data.id as string) ?? '',
    87→        sql: (data.sql as string) ?? '',
    88→        columns: (data.columns as string[]) ?? [],
    89→        rows: (data.rows as Record<string, unknown>[]) ?? [],
    90→        rowCount: (data.rowCount as number) ?? 0,
    91→        error: (data.error as string) ?? undefined,
    92→        rawContent: (data.content as string) ?? undefined,
    93→      };
    94→      callbacks.onToolResult(result);
    95→      break;
    96→    }
    97→    case 'done':
    98→      callbacks.onDone((data.session_id as string) ?? null);
    99→      break;
   100→    case 'error':
   101→      callbacks.onError((data.message as string) ?? 'Unknown error');
   102→      break;
   103→  }
   104→}
   105→
```

     1→"""Message parser for Claude Code SDK responses."""
     2→
     3→import logging
     4→from typing import Any
     5→
     6→from .._errors import MessageParseError
     7→from ..types import (
     8→    AssistantMessage,
     9→    ContentBlock,
    10→    Message,
    11→    ResultMessage,
    12→    StreamEvent,
    13→    SystemMessage,
    14→    TextBlock,
    15→    ThinkingBlock,
    16→    ToolResultBlock,
    17→    ToolUseBlock,
    18→    UserMessage,
    19→)
    20→
    21→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    22→
    23→
    24→def parse_message(data: dict[str, Any]) -> Message:
    25→    """
    26→    Parse message from CLI output into typed Message objects.
    27→
    28→    Args:
    29→        data: Raw message dictionary from CLI output
    30→
    31→    Returns:
    32→        Parsed Message object
    33→
    34→    Raises:
    35→        MessageParseError: If parsing fails or message type is unrecognized
    36→    """
    37→    if not isinstance(data, dict):
    38→        raise MessageParseError(
    39→            f"Invalid message data type (expected dict, got {type(data).__name__})",
    40→            data,
    41→        )
    42→
    43→    message_type = data.get("type")
    44→    if not message_type:
    45→        raise MessageParseError("Message missing 'type' field", data)
    46→
    47→    match message_type:
    48→        case "user":
    49→            try:
    50→                parent_tool_use_id = data.get("parent_tool_use_id")
    51→                tool_use_result = data.get("tool_use_result")
    52→                uuid = data.get("uuid")
    53→                if isinstance(data["message"]["content"], list):
    54→                    user_content_blocks: list[ContentBlock] = []
    55→                    for block in data["message"]["content"]:
    56→                        match block["type"]:
    57→                            case "text":
    58→                                user_content_blocks.append(
    59→                                    TextBlock(text=block["text"])
    60→                                )
    61→                            case "tool_use":
    62→                                user_content_blocks.append(
    63→                                    ToolUseBlock(
    64→                                        id=block["id"],
    65→                                        name=block["name"],
    66→                                        input=block["input"],
    67→                                    )
    68→                                )
    69→                            case "tool_result":
    70→                                user_content_blocks.append(
    71→                                    ToolResultBlock(
    72→                                        tool_use_id=block["tool_use_id"],
    73→                                        content=block.get("content"),
    74→                                        is_error=block.get("is_error"),
    75→                                    )
    76→                                )
    77→                    return UserMessage(
    78→                        content=user_content_blocks,
    79→                        uuid=uuid,
    80→                        parent_tool_use_id=parent_tool_use_id,
    81→                        tool_use_result=tool_use_result,
    82→                    )
    83→                return UserMessage(
    84→                    content=data["message"]["content"],
    85→                    uuid=uuid,
    86→                    parent_tool_use_id=parent_tool_use_id,
    87→                    tool_use_result=tool_use_result,
    88→                )
    89→            except KeyError as e:
    90→                raise MessageParseError(
    91→                    f"Missing required field in user message: {e}", data
    92→                ) from e
    93→
    94→        case "assistant":
    95→            try:
    96→                content_blocks: list[ContentBlock] = []
    97→                for block in data["message"]["content"]:
    98→                    match block["type"]:
    99→                        case "text":
   100→                            content_blocks.append(TextBlock(text=block["text"]))
   101→                        case "thinking":
   102→                            content_blocks.append(
   103→                                ThinkingBlock(
   104→                                    thinking=block["thinking"],
   105→                                    signature=block["signature"],
   106→                                )
   107→                            )
   108→                        case "tool_use":
   109→                            content_blocks.append(
   110→                                ToolUseBlock(
   111→                                    id=block["id"],
   112→                                    name=block["name"],
   113→                                    input=block["input"],
   114→                                )
   115→                            )
   116→                        case "tool_result":
   117→                            content_blocks.append(
   118→                                ToolResultBlock(
   119→                                    tool_use_id=block["tool_use_id"],
   120→                                    content=block.get("content"),
   121→                                    is_error=block.get("is_error"),
   122→                                )
   123→                            )
   124→
   125→                return AssistantMessage(
   126→                    content=content_blocks,
   127→                    model=data["message"]["model"],
   128→                    parent_tool_use_id=data.get("parent_tool_use_id"),
   129→                    error=data.get("error"),
   130→                )
   131→            except KeyError as e:
   132→                raise MessageParseError(
   133→                    f"Missing required field in assistant message: {e}", data
   134→                ) from e
   135→
   136→        case "system":
   137→            try:
   138→                return SystemMessage(
   139→                    subtype=data["subtype"],
   140→                    data=data,
   141→                )
   142→            except KeyError as e:
   143→                raise MessageParseError(
   144→                    f"Missing required field in system message: {e}", data
   145→                ) from e
   146→
   147→        case "result":
   148→            try:
   149→                return ResultMessage(
   150→                    subtype=data["subtype"],
   151→                    duration_ms=data["duration_ms"],
   152→                    duration_api_ms=data["duration_api_ms"],
   153→                    is_error=data["is_error"],
   154→                    num_turns=data["num_turns"],
   155→                    session_id=data["session_id"],
   156→                    total_cost_usd=data.get("total_cost_usd"),
   157→                    usage=data.get("usage"),
   158→                    result=data.get("result"),
   159→                    structured_output=data.get("structured_output"),
   160→                )
   161→            except KeyError as e:
   162→                raise MessageParseError(
   163→                    f"Missing required field in result message: {e}", data
   164→                ) from e
   165→
   166→        case "stream_event":
   167→            try:
   168→                return StreamEvent(
   169→                    uuid=data["uuid"],
   170→                    session_id=data["session_id"],
   171→                    event=data["event"],
   172→                    parent_tool_use_id=data.get("parent_tool_use_id"),
   173→                )
   174→            except KeyError as e:
   175→                raise MessageParseError(
   176→                    f"Missing required field in stream_event message: {e}", data
   177→                ) from e
   178→
   179→        case _:
   180→            raise MessageParseError(f"Unknown message type: {message_type}", data)
   181→

    68→@dataclass
    69→class PermissionUpdate:
    70→    """Permission update configuration."""
    71→
    72→    type: Literal[
    73→        "addRules",
    74→        "replaceRules",
    75→        "removeRules",
    76→        "setMode",
    77→        "addDirectories",
    78→        "removeDirectories",
    79→    ]
    80→    rules: list[PermissionRuleValue] | None = None
    81→    behavior: PermissionBehavior | None = None
    82→    mode: PermissionMode | None = None
    83→    directories: list[str] | None = None
    84→    destination: PermissionUpdateDestination | None = None
    85→
    86→    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
    87→        """Convert PermissionUpdate to dictionary format matching TypeScript control protocol."""
    88→        result: dict[str, Any] = {
    89→            "type": self.type,
    90→        }
    91→
    92→        # Add destination for all variants
    93→        if self.destination is not None:
    94→            result["destination"] = self.destination
    95→
    96→        # Handle different type variants
    97→        if self.type in ["addRules", "replaceRules", "removeRules"]:
    98→            # Rules-based variants require rules and behavior
    99→            if self.rules is not None:
   100→                result["rules"] = [
   101→                    {
   102→                        "toolName": rule.tool_name,
   103→                        "ruleContent": rule.rule_content,
   104→                    }
   105→                    for rule in self.rules
   106→                ]
   107→            if self.behavior is not None:
   108→                result["behavior"] = self.behavior
   109→
   110→        elif self.type == "setMode":
   111→            # Mode variant requires mode
   112→            if self.mode is not None:
   113→                result["mode"] = self.mode
   114→
   115→        elif self.type in ["addDirectories", "removeDirectories"]:
   116→            # Directory variants require directories
   117→            if self.directories is not None:
   118→                result["directories"] = self.directories
   119→
   120→        return result

   361→# See https://docs.anthropic.com/en/docs/claude-code/hooks#advanced%3A-json-output
   362→# for documentation of the output types.
   363→#
   364→# IMPORTANT: The Python SDK uses `async_` and `continue_` (with underscores) to avoid
   365→# Python keyword conflicts. These fields are automatically converted to `async` and
   366→# `continue` when sent to the CLI. You should use the underscore versions in your
   367→# Python code.
   368→class AsyncHookJSONOutput(TypedDict):
   369→    """Async hook output that defers hook execution.
   370→
   371→    Fields:
   372→        async_: Set to True to defer hook execution. Note: This is converted to
   373→            "async" when sent to the CLI - use "async_" in your Python code.
   374→        asyncTimeout: Optional timeout in milliseconds for the async operation.
   375→    """
   376→
   377→    async_: Literal[
   378→        True
   379→    ]  # Using async_ to avoid Python keyword (converted to "async" for CLI)
   380→    asyncTimeout: NotRequired[int]
   381→
   382→
   383→class SyncHookJSONOutput(TypedDict):
   384→    """Synchronous hook output with control and decision fields.
   385→
   386→    This defines the structure for hook callbacks to control execution and provide
   387→    feedback to Claude.
   388→
   389→    Common Control Fields:
   390→        continue_: Whether Claude should proceed after hook execution (default: True).
   391→            Note: This is converted to "continue" when sent to the CLI.
   392→        suppressOutput: Hide stdout from transcript mode (default: False).
   393→        stopReason: Message shown when continue is False.
   394→
   395→    Decision Fields:
   396→        decision: Set to "block" to indicate blocking behavior.
   397→        systemMessage: Warning message displayed to the user.
   398→        reason: Feedback message for Claude about the decision.
   399→
   400→    Hook-Specific Output:
   401→        hookSpecificOutput: Event-specific controls (e.g., permissionDecision for
   402→            PreToolUse, additionalContext for PostToolUse).
   403→
   404→    Note: The CLI documentation shows field names without underscores ("async", "continue"),
   405→    but Python code should use the underscore versions ("async_", "continue_") as they
   406→    are automatically converted.
   407→    """
   408→
   409→    # Common control fields
   410→    continue_: NotRequired[
   411→        bool
   412→    ]  # Using continue_ to avoid Python keyword (converted to "continue" for CLI)
   413→    suppressOutput: NotRequired[bool]
   414→    stopReason: NotRequired[str]
   415→
   416→    # Decision fields
   417→    # Note: "approve" is deprecated for PreToolUse (use permissionDecision instead)
   418→    # For other hooks, only "block" is meaningful
   419→    decision: NotRequired[Literal["block"]]
   420→    systemMessage: NotRequired[str]
   421→    reason: NotRequired[str]
   422→
   423→    # Hook-specific outputs
   424→    hookSpecificOutput: NotRequired[HookSpecificOutput]
   425→
   426→
   427→HookJSONOutput = AsyncHookJSONOutput | SyncHookJSONOutput
   428→
   429→
   430→class HookContext(TypedDict):
   431→    """Context information for hook callbacks.
   432→
   433→    Fields:
   434→        signal: Reserved for future abort signal support. Currently always None.
   435→    """
   436→
   437→    signal: Any | None  # Future: abort signal support
   438→
   439→
   440→HookCallback = Callable[
   441→    # HookCallback input parameters:
   442→    # - input: Strongly-typed hook input with discriminated unions based on hook_event_name
   443→    # - tool_use_id: Optional tool use identifier
   444→    # - context: Hook context with abort signal support (currently placeholder)
   445→    [HookInput, str | None, HookContext],
   446→    Awaitable[HookJSONOutput],
   447→]

   548→class SandboxSettings(TypedDict, total=False):
   549→    """Sandbox settings configuration.
   550→
   551→    This controls how Claude Code sandboxes bash commands for filesystem
   552→    and network isolation.
   553→
   554→    **Important:** Filesystem and network restrictions are configured via permission
   555→    rules, not via these sandbox settings:
   556→    - Filesystem read restrictions: Use Read deny rules
   557→    - Filesystem write restrictions: Use Edit allow/deny rules
   558→    - Network restrictions: Use WebFetch allow/deny rules
   559→
   560→    Attributes:
   561→        enabled: Enable bash sandboxing (macOS/Linux only). Default: False
   562→        autoAllowBashIfSandboxed: Auto-approve bash commands when sandboxed. Default: True
   563→        excludedCommands: Commands that should run outside the sandbox (e.g., ["git", "docker"])
   564→        allowUnsandboxedCommands: Allow commands to bypass sandbox via dangerouslyDisableSandbox.
   565→            When False, all commands must run sandboxed (or be in excludedCommands). Default: True
   566→        network: Network configuration for sandbox.
   567→        ignoreViolations: Violations to ignore.
   568→        enableWeakerNestedSandbox: Enable weaker sandbox for unprivileged Docker environments
   569→            (Linux only). Reduces security. Default: False
   570→
   571→    Example:
   572→        ```python
   573→        sandbox_settings: SandboxSettings = {
   574→            "enabled": True,
   575→            "autoAllowBashIfSandboxed": True,
   576→            "excludedCommands": ["docker"],
   577→            "network": {
   578→                "allowUnixSockets": ["/var/run/docker.sock"],
   579→                "allowLocalBinding": True
   580→            }
   581→        }
   582→        ```
   583→    """
   584→
   585→    enabled: bool
