```
     1→"""Query class for handling bidirectional control protocol."""
     2→
     3→import json
     4→import logging
     5→import os
     6→from collections.abc import AsyncIterable, AsyncIterator, Awaitable, Callable
     7→from contextlib import suppress
     8→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any
     9→
    10→import anyio
    11→from mcp.types import (
    12→    CallToolRequest,
    13→    CallToolRequestParams,
    14→    ListToolsRequest,
    15→)
    16→
    17→from ..types import (
    18→    PermissionResultAllow,
    19→    PermissionResultDeny,
    20→    SDKControlPermissionRequest,
    21→    SDKControlRequest,
    22→    SDKControlResponse,
    23→    SDKHookCallbackRequest,
    24→    ToolPermissionContext,
    25→)
    26→from .transport import Transport
    27→
    28→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    29→    from mcp.server import Server as McpServer
    30→
    31→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    32→
    33→
    34→def _convert_hook_output_for_cli(hook_output: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    35→    """Convert Python-safe field names to CLI-expected field names.
    36→
    37→    The Python SDK uses `async_` and `continue_` to avoid keyword conflicts,
    38→    but the CLI expects `async` and `continue`. This function performs the
    39→    necessary conversion.
    40→    """
    41→    converted = {}
    42→    for key, value in hook_output.items():
    43→        # Convert Python-safe names to JavaScript names
    44→        if key == "async_":
    45→            converted["async"] = value
    46→        elif key == "continue_":
    47→            converted["continue"] = value
    48→        else:
    49→            converted[key] = value
    50→    return converted
    51→
    52→
    53→class Query:
    54→    """Handles bidirectional control protocol on top of Transport.
    55→
    56→    This class manages:
    57→    - Control request/response routing
    58→    - Hook callbacks
    59→    - Tool permission callbacks
    60→    - Message streaming
    61→    - Initialization handshake
    62→    """
    63→
    64→    def __init__(
    65→        self,
    66→        transport: Transport,
    67→        is_streaming_mode: bool,
    68→        can_use_tool: Callable[
    69→            [str, dict[str, Any], ToolPermissionContext],
    70→            Awaitable[PermissionResultAllow | PermissionResultDeny],
    71→        ]
    72→        | None = None,
    73→        hooks: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] | None = None,
    74→        sdk_mcp_servers: dict[str, "McpServer"] | None = None,
    75→        initialize_timeout: float = 60.0,
    76→        agents: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
    77→    ):
    78→        """Initialize Query with transport and callbacks.
    79→
    80→        Args:
    81→            transport: Low-level transport for I/O
    82→            is_streaming_mode: Whether using streaming (bidirectional) mode
    83→            can_use_tool: Optional callback for tool permission requests
    84→            hooks: Optional hook configurations
    85→            sdk_mcp_servers: Optional SDK MCP server instances
    86→            initialize_timeout: Timeout in seconds for the initialize request
    87→            agents: Optional agent definitions to send via initialize
    88→        """
    89→        self._initialize_timeout = initialize_timeout
    90→        self.transport = transport
    91→        self.is_streaming_mode = is_streaming_mode
    92→        self.can_use_tool = can_use_tool
    93→        self.hooks = hooks or {}
    94→        self.sdk_mcp_servers = sdk_mcp_servers or {}
    95→        self._agents = agents
    96→
    97→        # Control protocol state
    98→        self.pending_control_responses: dict[str, anyio.Event] = {}
    99→        self.pending_control_results: dict[str, dict[str, Any] | Exception] = {}
   100→        self.hook_callbacks: dict[str, Callable[..., Any]] = {}
   101→        self.next_callback_id = 0
   102→        self._request_counter = 0
   103→
   104→        # Message stream
   105→        self._message_send, self._message_receive = anyio.create_memory_object_stream[
   106→            dict[str, Any]
   107→        ](max_buffer_size=100)
   108→        self._tg: anyio.abc.TaskGroup | None = None
   109→        self._initialized = False
   110→        self._closed = False
   111→        self._initialization_result: dict[str, Any] | None = None
   112→
   113→        # Track first result for proper stream closure with SDK MCP servers
   114→        self._first_result_event = anyio.Event()
   115→        self._stream_close_timeout = (
   116→            float(os.environ.get("CLAUDE_CODE_STREAM_CLOSE_TIMEOUT", "60000")) / 1000.0
   117→        )  # Convert ms to seconds
   118→
   119→    async def initialize(self) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
   120→        """Initialize control protocol if in streaming mode.
   121→
   122→        Returns:
   123→            Initialize response with supported commands, or None if not streaming
   124→        """
   125→        if not self.is_streaming_mode:
   126→            return None
   127→
   128→        # Build hooks configuration for initialization
   129→        hooks_config: dict[str, Any] = {}
   130→        if self.hooks:
   131→            for event, matchers in self.hooks.items():
   132→                if matchers:
   133→                    hooks_config[event] = []
   134→                    for matcher in matchers:
   135→                        callback_ids = []
   136→                        for callback in matcher.get("hooks", []):
   137→                            callback_id = f"hook_{self.next_callback_id}"
   138→                            self.next_callback_id += 1
   139→                            self.hook_callbacks[callback_id] = callback
   140→                            callback_ids.append(callback_id)
   141→                        hook_matcher_config: dict[str, Any] = {
   142→                            "matcher": matcher.get("matcher"),
   143→                            "hookCallbackIds": callback_ids,
   144→                        }
   145→                        if matcher.get("timeout") is not None:
   146→                            hook_matcher_config["timeout"] = matcher.get("timeout")
   147→                        hooks_config[event].append(hook_matcher_config)
   148→
   149→        # Send initialize request
   150→        request: dict[str, Any] = {
   151→            "subtype": "initialize",
   152→            "hooks": hooks_config if hooks_config else None,
   153→        }
   154→        if self._agents:
   155→            request["agents"] = self._agents
   156→
   157→        # Use longer timeout for initialize since MCP servers may take time to start
   158→        response = await self._send_control_request(
   159→            request, timeout=self._initialize_timeout
   160→        )
   161→        self._initialized = True
   162→        self._initialization_result = response  # Store for later access
   163→        return response
   164→
   165→    async def start(self) -> None:
   166→        """Start reading messages from transport."""
   167→        if self._tg is None:
   168→            self._tg = anyio.create_task_group()
   169→            await self._tg.__aenter__()
   170→            self._tg.start_soon(self._read_messages)
   171→
   172→    async def _read_messages(self) -> None:
   173→        """Read messages from transport and route them."""
   174→        try:
   175→            async for message in self.transport.read_messages():
   176→                if self._closed:
   177→                    break
   178→
   179→                msg_type = message.get("type")
   180→
   181→                # Route control messages
   182→                if msg_type == "control_response":
   183→                    response = message.get("response", {})
   184→                    request_id = response.get("request_id")
   185→                    if request_id in self.pending_control_responses:
   186→                        event = self.pending_control_responses[request_id]
   187→                        if response.get("subtype") == "error":
   188→                            self.pending_control_results[request_id] = Exception(
   189→                                response.get("error", "Unknown error")
   190→                            )
   191→                        else:
   192→                            self.pending_control_results[request_id] = response
   193→                        event.set()
   194→                    continue
   195→
   196→                elif msg_type == "control_request":
   197→                    # Handle incoming control requests from CLI
   198→                    # Cast message to SDKControlRequest for type safety
   199→                    request: SDKControlRequest = message  # type: ignore[assignment]
   200→                    if self._tg:
   201→                        self._tg.start_soon(self._handle_control_request, request)
   202→                    continue
   203→
   204→                elif msg_type == "control_cancel_request":
   205→                    # Handle cancel requests
   206→                    # TODO: Implement cancellation support
   207→                    continue
   208→
   209→                # Track results for proper stream closure
   210→                if msg_type == "result":
   211→                    self._first_result_event.set()
   212→
   213→                # Regular SDK messages go to the stream
   214→                await self._message_send.send(message)
   215→
   216→        except anyio.get_cancelled_exc_class():
   217→            # Task was cancelled - this is expected behavior
   218→            logger.debug("Read task cancelled")
   219→            raise  # Re-raise to properly handle cancellation
   220→        except Exception as e:
   221→            logger.error(f"Fatal error in message reader: {e}")
   222→            # Signal all pending control requests so they fail fast instead of timing out
   223→            for request_id, event in list(self.pending_control_responses.items()):
   224→                if request_id not in self.pending_control_results:
   225→                    self.pending_control_results[request_id] = e
   226→                    event.set()
   227→            # Put error in stream so iterators can handle it
   228→            await self._message_send.send({"type": "error", "error": str(e)})
   229→        finally:
   230→            # Always signal end of stream
   231→            await self._message_send.send({"type": "end"})
   232→
   233→    async def _handle_control_request(self, request: SDKControlRequest) -> None:
   234→        """Handle incoming control request from CLI."""
   235→        request_id = request["request_id"]
   236→        request_data = request["request"]
   237→        subtype = request_data["subtype"]
   238→
   239→        try:
   240→            response_data: dict[str, Any] = {}
   241→
   242→            if subtype == "can_use_tool":
   243→                permission_request: SDKControlPermissionRequest = request_data  # type: ignore[assignment]
   244→                original_input = permission_request["input"]
   245→                # Handle tool permission request
   246→                if not self.can_use_tool:
   247→                    raise Exception("canUseTool callback is not provided")
   248→
   249→                context = ToolPermissionContext(
   250→                    signal=None,  # TODO: Add abort signal support
   251→                    suggestions=permission_request.get("permission_suggestions", [])
   252→                    or [],
   253→                )
   254→
   255→                response = await self.can_use_tool(
   256→                    permission_request["tool_name"],
   257→                    permission_request["input"],
   258→                    context,
   259→                )
   260→
   261→                # Convert PermissionResult to expected dict format
   262→                if isinstance(response, PermissionResultAllow):
   263→                    response_data = {
   264→                        "behavior": "allow",
   265→                        "updatedInput": (
   266→                            response.updated_input
   267→                            if response.updated_input is not None
   268→                            else original_input
   269→                        ),
   270→                    }
   271→                    if response.updated_permissions is not None:
   272→                        response_data["updatedPermissions"] = [
   273→                            permission.to_dict()
   274→                            for permission in response.updated_permissions
   275→                        ]
   276→                elif isinstance(response, PermissionResultDeny):
   277→                    response_data = {"behavior": "deny", "message": response.message}
   278→                    if response.interrupt:
   279→                        response_data["interrupt"] = response.interrupt
   280→                else:
   281→                    raise TypeError(
   282→                        f"Tool permission callback must return PermissionResult (PermissionResultAllow or PermissionResultDeny), got {type(response)}"
   283→                    )
   284→
   285→            elif subtype == "hook_callback":
   286→                hook_callback_request: SDKHookCallbackRequest = request_data  # type: ignore[assignment]
   287→                # Handle hook callback
   288→                callback_id = hook_callback_request["callback_id"]
   289→                callback = self.hook_callbacks.get(callback_id)
   290→                if not callback:
   291→                    raise Exception(f"No hook callback found for ID: {callback_id}")
   292→
   293→                hook_output = await callback(
   294→                    request_data.get("input"),
   295→                    request_data.get("tool_use_id"),
   296→                    {"signal": None},  # TODO: Add abort signal support
   297→                )
   298→                # Convert Python-safe field names (async_, continue_) to CLI-expected names (async, continue)
   299→                response_data = _convert_hook_output_for_cli(hook_output)
   300→
   301→            elif subtype == "mcp_message":
   302→                # Handle SDK MCP request
   303→                server_name = request_data.get("server_name")
   304→                mcp_message = request_data.get("message")
   305→
   306→                if not server_name or not mcp_message:
   307→                    raise Exception("Missing server_name or message for MCP request")
   308→
   309→                # Type narrowing - we've verified these are not None above
   310→                assert isinstance(server_name, str)
   311→                assert isinstance(mcp_message, dict)
   312→                mcp_response = await self._handle_sdk_mcp_request(
   313→                    server_name, mcp_message
   314→                )
   315→                # Wrap the MCP response as expected by the control protocol
   316→                response_data = {"mcp_response": mcp_response}
   317→
   318→            else:
   319→                raise Exception(f"Unsupported control request subtype: {subtype}")
   320→
   321→            # Send success response
   322→            success_response: SDKControlResponse = {
   323→                "type": "control_response",
   324→                "response": {
   325→                    "subtype": "success",
   326→                    "request_id": request_id,
   327→                    "response": response_data,
   328→                },
   329→            }
   330→            await self.transport.write(json.dumps(success_response) + "\n")
   331→
   332→        except Exception as e:
   333→            # Send error response
   334→            error_response: SDKControlResponse = {
   335→                "type": "control_response",
   336→                "response": {
   337→                    "subtype": "error",
   338→                    "request_id": request_id,
   339→                    "error": str(e),
   340→                },
   341→            }
   342→            await self.transport.write(json.dumps(error_response) + "\n")
   343→
   344→    async def _send_control_request(
   345→        self, request: dict[str, Any], timeout: float = 60.0
   346→    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
   347→        """Send control request to CLI and wait for response.
   348→
   349→        Args:
   350→            request: The control request to send
   351→            timeout: Timeout in seconds to wait for response (default 60s)
   352→        """
   353→        if not self.is_streaming_mode:
   354→            raise Exception("Control requests require streaming mode")
   355→
   356→        # Generate unique request ID
   357→        self._request_counter += 1
   358→        request_id = f"req_{self._request_counter}_{os.urandom(4).hex()}"
   359→
   360→        # Create event for response
   361→        event = anyio.Event()
   362→        self.pending_control_responses[request_id] = event
   363→
   364→        # Build and send request
   365→        control_request = {
   366→            "type": "control_request",
   367→            "request_id": request_id,
   368→            "request": request,
   369→        }
   370→
   371→        await self.transport.write(json.dumps(control_request) + "\n")
   372→
   373→        # Wait for response
   374→        try:
   375→            with anyio.fail_after(timeout):
   376→                await event.wait()
   377→
   378→            result = self.pending_control_results.pop(request_id)
   379→            self.pending_control_responses.pop(request_id, None)
   380→
   381→            if isinstance(result, Exception):
   382→                raise result
   383→
   384→            response_data = result.get("response", {})
   385→            return response_data if isinstance(response_data, dict) else {}
   386→        except TimeoutError as e:
   387→            self.pending_control_responses.pop(request_id, None)
   388→            self.pending_control_results.pop(request_id, None)
   389→            raise Exception(f"Control request timeout: {request.get('subtype')}") from e
   390→
   391→    async def _handle_sdk_mcp_request(
   392→        self, server_name: str, message: dict[str, Any]
   393→    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
   394→        """Handle an MCP request for an SDK server.
   395→
   396→        This acts as a bridge between JSONRPC messages from the CLI
   397→        and the in-process MCP server. Ideally the MCP SDK would provide
   398→        a method to handle raw JSONRPC, but for now we route manually.
   399→
   400→        Args:
   401→            server_name: Name of the SDK MCP server
   402→            message: The JSONRPC message
   403→
   404→        Returns:
   405→            The response message
   406→        """
   407→        if server_name not in self.sdk_mcp_servers:
   408→            return {
   409→                "jsonrpc": "2.0",
   410→                "id": message.get("id"),
   411→                "error": {
   412→                    "code": -32601,
   413→                    "message": f"Server '{server_name}' not found",
   414→                },
   415→            }
   416→
   417→        server = self.sdk_mcp_servers[server_name]
   418→        method = message.get("method")
   419→        params = message.get("params", {})
   420→
   421→        try:
   422→            # TODO: Python MCP SDK lacks the Transport abstraction that TypeScript has.
   423→            # TypeScript: server.connect(transport) allows custom transports
   424→            # Python: server.run(read_stream, write_stream) requires actual streams
   425→            #
   426→            # This forces us to manually route methods. When Python MCP adds Transport
   427→            # support, we can refactor to match the TypeScript approach.
   428→            if method == "initialize":
   429→                # Handle MCP initialization - hardcoded for tools only, no listChanged
   430→                return {
   431→                    "jsonrpc": "2.0",
   432→                    "id": message.get("id"),
   433→                    "result": {
   434→                        "protocolVersion": "2024-11-05",
   435→                        "capabilities": {
   436→                            "tools": {}  # Tools capability without listChanged
   437→                        },
   438→                        "serverInfo": {
   439→                            "name": server.name,
   440→                            "version": server.version or "1.0.0",
   441→                        },
   442→                    },
   443→                }
   444→
   445→            elif method == "tools/list":
   446→                request = ListToolsRequest(method=method)
   447→                handler = server.request_handlers.get(ListToolsRequest)
   448→                if handler:
   449→                    result = await handler(request)
   450→                    # Convert MCP result to JSONRPC response
   451→                    tools_data = []
   452→                    for tool in result.root.tools:  # type: ignore[union-attr]
   453→                        tool_data: dict[str, Any] = {
   454→                            "name": tool.name,
   455→                            "description": tool.description,
   456→                            "inputSchema": (
   457→                                tool.inputSchema.model_dump()
   458→                                if hasattr(tool.inputSchema, "model_dump")
   459→                                else tool.inputSchema
   460→                            )
   461→                            if tool.inputSchema
   462→                            else {},
   463→                        }
   464→                        if tool.annotations:
   465→                            tool_data["annotations"] = tool.annotations.model_dump(
   466→                                exclude_none=True
   467→                            )
   468→                        tools_data.append(tool_data)
   469→                    return {
   470→                        "jsonrpc": "2.0",
   471→                        "id": message.get("id"),
   472→                        "result": {"tools": tools_data},
   473→                    }
   474→
   475→            elif method == "tools/call":
   476→                call_request = CallToolRequest(
   477→                    method=method,
   478→                    params=CallToolRequestParams(
   479→                        name=params.get("name"), arguments=params.get("arguments", {})
   480→                    ),
   481→                )
   482→                handler = server.request_handlers.get(CallToolRequest)
   483→                if handler:
   484→                    result = await handler(call_request)
   485→                    # Convert MCP result to JSONRPC response
   486→                    content = []
   487→                    for item in result.root.content:  # type: ignore[union-attr]
   488→                        if hasattr(item, "text"):
   489→                            content.append({"type": "text", "text": item.text})
   490→                        elif hasattr(item, "data") and hasattr(item, "mimeType"):
   491→                            content.append(
   492→                                {
   493→                                    "type": "image",
   494→                                    "data": item.data,
   495→                                    "mimeType": item.mimeType,
   496→                                }
   497→                            )
   498→
   499→                    response_data = {"content": content}
   500→                    if hasattr(result.root, "is_error") and result.root.is_error:
   501→                        response_data["is_error"] = True  # type: ignore[assignment]
   502→
   503→                    return {
   504→                        "jsonrpc": "2.0",
   505→                        "id": message.get("id"),
   506→                        "result": response_data,
   507→                    }
   508→
   509→            elif method == "notifications/initialized":
   510→                # Handle initialized notification - just acknowledge it
   511→                return {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "result": {}}
   512→
   513→            # Add more methods here as MCP SDK adds them (resources, prompts, etc.)
   514→            # This is the limitation Ashwin pointed out - we have to manually update
   515→
   516→            return {
   517→                "jsonrpc": "2.0",
   518→                "id": message.get("id"),
   519→                "error": {"code": -32601, "message": f"Method '{method}' not found"},
   520→            }
   521→
   522→        except Exception as e:
   523→            return {
   524→                "jsonrpc": "2.0",
   525→                "id": message.get("id"),
   526→                "error": {"code": -32603, "message": str(e)},
   527→            }
   528→
   529→    async def get_mcp_status(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
   530→        """Get current MCP server connection status."""
   531→        return await self._send_control_request({"subtype": "mcp_status"})
   532→
   533→    async def interrupt(self) -> None:
   534→        """Send interrupt control request."""
   535→        await self._send_control_request({"subtype": "interrupt"})
   536→
   537→    async def set_permission_mode(self, mode: str) -> None:
   538→        """Change permission mode."""
   539→        await self._send_control_request(
   540→            {
   541→                "subtype": "set_permission_mode",
   542→                "mode": mode,
   543→            }
   544→        )
   545→
   546→    async def set_model(self, model: str | None) -> None:
   547→        """Change the AI model."""
   548→        await self._send_control_request(
   549→            {
   550→                "subtype": "set_model",
   551→                "model": model,
   552→            }
   553→        )
   554→
   555→    async def rewind_files(self, user_message_id: str) -> None:
   556→        """Rewind tracked files to their state at a specific user message.
   557→
   558→        Requires file checkpointing to be enabled via the `enable_file_checkpointing` option.
   559→
   560→        Args:
   561→            user_message_id: UUID of the user message to rewind to
   562→        """
   563→        await self._send_control_request(
   564→            {
   565→                "subtype": "rewind_files",
   566→                "user_message_id": user_message_id,
   567→            }
   568→        )
   569→
   570→    async def stream_input(self, stream: AsyncIterable[dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
   571→        """Stream input messages to transport.
   572→
   573→        If SDK MCP servers or hooks are present, waits for the first result
   574→        before closing stdin to allow bidirectional control protocol communication.
   575→        """
   576→        try:
   577→            async for message in stream:
   578→                if self._closed:
   579→                    break
   580→                await self.transport.write(json.dumps(message) + "\n")
   581→
   582→            # If we have SDK MCP servers or hooks that need bidirectional communication,
   583→            # wait for first result before closing the channel
   584→            has_hooks = bool(self.hooks)
   585→            if self.sdk_mcp_servers or has_hooks:
   586→                logger.debug(
   587→                    f"Waiting for first result before closing stdin "
   588→                    f"(sdk_mcp_servers={len(self.sdk_mcp_servers)}, has_hooks={has_hooks})"
   589→                )
   590→                try:
   591→                    with anyio.move_on_after(self._stream_close_timeout):
   592→                        await self._first_result_event.wait()
   593→                        logger.debug("Received first result, closing input stream")
   594→                except Exception:
   595→                    logger.debug(
   596→                        "Timed out waiting for first result, closing input stream"
   597→                    )
   598→
   599→            # After all messages sent (and result received if needed), end input
   600→            await self.transport.end_input()
   601→        except Exception as e:
   602→            logger.debug(f"Error streaming input: {e}")
   603→
   604→    async def receive_messages(self) -> AsyncIterator[dict[str, Any]]:
   605→        """Receive SDK messages (not control messages)."""
   606→        async for message in self._message_receive:
   607→            # Check for special messages
   608→            if message.get("type") == "end":
   609→                break
   610→            elif message.get("type") == "error":
   611→                raise Exception(message.get("error", "Unknown error"))
   612→
   613→            yield message
   614→
   615→    async def close(self) -> None:
   616→        """Close the query and transport."""
   617→        self._closed = True
   618→        if self._tg:
   619→            self._tg.cancel_scope.cancel()
   620→            # Wait for task group to complete cancellation
   621→            with suppress(anyio.get_cancelled_exc_class()):
   622→                await self._tg.__aexit__(None, None, None)
   623→        await self.transport.close()
   624→
   625→    # Make Query an async iterator
   626→    def __aiter__(self) -> AsyncIterator[dict[str, Any]]:
   627→        """Return async iterator for messages."""
   628→        return self.receive_messages()
   629→
   630→    async def __anext__(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
   631→        """Get next message."""
   632→        async for message in self.receive_messages():
   633→            return message
   634→        raise StopAsyncIteration
   635→
```

```
     1→"""Internal client implementation."""
     2→
     3→from collections.abc import AsyncIterable, AsyncIterator
     4→from dataclasses import asdict, replace
     5→from typing import Any
     6→
     7→from ..types import (
     8→    ClaudeAgentOptions,
     9→    HookEvent,
    10→    HookMatcher,
    11→    Message,
    12→)
    13→from .message_parser import parse_message
    14→from .query import Query
    15→from .transport import Transport
    16→from .transport.subprocess_cli import SubprocessCLITransport
    17→
    18→
    19→class InternalClient:
    20→    """Internal client implementation."""
    21→
    22→    def __init__(self) -> None:
    23→        """Initialize the internal client."""
    24→
    25→    def _convert_hooks_to_internal_format(
    26→        self, hooks: dict[HookEvent, list[HookMatcher]]
    27→    ) -> dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]]:
    28→        """Convert HookMatcher format to internal Query format."""
    29→        internal_hooks: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = {}
    30→        for event, matchers in hooks.items():
    31→            internal_hooks[event] = []
    32→            for matcher in matchers:
    33→                # Convert HookMatcher to internal dict format
    34→                internal_matcher: dict[str, Any] = {
    35→                    "matcher": matcher.matcher if hasattr(matcher, "matcher") else None,
    36→                    "hooks": matcher.hooks if hasattr(matcher, "hooks") else [],
    37→                }
    38→                if hasattr(matcher, "timeout") and matcher.timeout is not None:
    39→                    internal_matcher["timeout"] = matcher.timeout
    40→                internal_hooks[event].append(internal_matcher)
    41→        return internal_hooks
    42→
    43→    async def process_query(
    44→        self,
    45→        prompt: str | AsyncIterable[dict[str, Any]],
    46→        options: ClaudeAgentOptions,
    47→        transport: Transport | None = None,
    48→    ) -> AsyncIterator[Message]:
    49→        """Process a query through transport and Query."""
    50→
    51→        # Validate and configure permission settings (matching TypeScript SDK logic)
    52→        configured_options = options
    53→        if options.can_use_tool:
    54→            # canUseTool callback requires streaming mode (AsyncIterable prompt)
    55→            if isinstance(prompt, str):
    56→                raise ValueError(
    57→                    "can_use_tool callback requires streaming mode. "
    58→                    "Please provide prompt as an AsyncIterable instead of a string."
    59→                )
    60→
    61→            # canUseTool and permission_prompt_tool_name are mutually exclusive
    62→            if options.permission_prompt_tool_name:
    63→                raise ValueError(
    64→                    "can_use_tool callback cannot be used with permission_prompt_tool_name. "
    65→                    "Please use one or the other."
    66→                )
    67→
    68→            # Automatically set permission_prompt_tool_name to "stdio" for control protocol
    69→            configured_options = replace(options, permission_prompt_tool_name="stdio")
    70→
    71→        # Use provided transport or create subprocess transport
    72→        if transport is not None:
    73→            chosen_transport = transport
    74→        else:
    75→            chosen_transport = SubprocessCLITransport(
    76→                prompt=prompt,
    77→                options=configured_options,
    78→            )
    79→
    80→        # Connect transport
    81→        await chosen_transport.connect()
    82→
    83→        # Extract SDK MCP servers from configured options
    84→        sdk_mcp_servers = {}
    85→        if configured_options.mcp_servers and isinstance(
    86→            configured_options.mcp_servers, dict
    87→        ):
    88→            for name, config in configured_options.mcp_servers.items():
    89→                if isinstance(config, dict) and config.get("type") == "sdk":
    90→                    sdk_mcp_servers[name] = config["instance"]  # type: ignore[typeddict-item]
    91→
    92→        # Convert agents to dict format for initialize request
    93→        agents_dict = None
    94→        if configured_options.agents:
    95→            agents_dict = {
    96→                name: {k: v for k, v in asdict(agent_def).items() if v is not None}
    97→                for name, agent_def in configured_options.agents.items()
    98→            }
    99→
   100→        # Create Query to handle control protocol
   101→        # Always use streaming mode internally (matching TypeScript SDK)
   102→        # This ensures agents are always sent via initialize request
   103→        query = Query(
   104→            transport=chosen_transport,
   105→            is_streaming_mode=True,  # Always streaming internally
   106→            can_use_tool=configured_options.can_use_tool,
   107→            hooks=self._convert_hooks_to_internal_format(configured_options.hooks)
   108→            if configured_options.hooks
   109→            else None,
   110→            sdk_mcp_servers=sdk_mcp_servers,
   111→            agents=agents_dict,
   112→        )
   113→
   114→        try:
   115→            # Start reading messages
   116→            await query.start()
   117→
   118→            # Always initialize to send agents via stdin (matching TypeScript SDK)
   119→            await query.initialize()
   120→
   121→            # Handle prompt input
   122→            if isinstance(prompt, str):
   123→                # For string prompts, write user message to stdin after initialize
   124→                # (matching TypeScript SDK behavior)
   125→                import json
   126→
   127→                user_message = {
   128→                    "type": "user",
   129→                    "session_id": "",
   130→                    "message": {"role": "user", "content": prompt},
   131→                    "parent_tool_use_id": None,
   132→                }
   133→                await chosen_transport.write(json.dumps(user_message) + "\n")
   134→                await chosen_transport.end_input()
   135→            elif isinstance(prompt, AsyncIterable) and query._tg:
   136→                # Stream input in background for async iterables
   137→                query._tg.start_soon(query.stream_input, prompt)
   138→
   139→            # Yield parsed messages
   140→            async for data in query.receive_messages():
   141→                yield parse_message(data)
   142→
   143→        finally:
   144→            await query.close()
   145→
```

```
     1→"""Claude SDK for Python."""
     2→
     3→from collections.abc import Awaitable, Callable
     4→from dataclasses import dataclass
     5→from typing import Any, Generic, TypeVar
     6→
     7→from mcp.types import ToolAnnotations
     8→
     9→from ._errors import (
    10→    ClaudeSDKError,
    11→    CLIConnectionError,
    12→    CLIJSONDecodeError,
    13→    CLINotFoundError,
    14→    ProcessError,
    15→)
    16→from ._internal.transport import Transport
    17→from ._version import __version__
    18→from .client import ClaudeSDKClient
    19→from .query import query
    20→from .types import (
    21→    AgentDefinition,
    22→    AssistantMessage,
    23→    BaseHookInput,

    40→    PermissionResult,

    42→    PermissionResultDeny,

    62→    ThinkingBlock,
    63→    ThinkingConfig,
    64→    ThinkingConfigAdaptive,
    65→    ThinkingConfigDisabled,
    66→    ThinkingConfigEnabled,
    67→    ToolPermissionContext,
    68→    ToolResultBlock,
    69→    ToolUseBlock,
    70→    UserMessage,
    71→    UserPromptSubmitHookInput,
    72→)
    73→
    74→# MCP Server Support
    75→
    76→T = TypeVar("T")
    77→
    78→
    79→@dataclass
    80→class SdkMcpTool(Generic[T]):
    81→    """Definition for an SDK MCP tool."""
    82→
    83→    name: str
    84→    description: str
    85→    input_schema: type[T] | dict[str, Any]
    86→    handler: Callable[[T], Awaitable[dict[str, Any]]]
    87→    annotations: ToolAnnotations | None = None
    88→
    89→
    90→def tool(
    91→    name: str,
    92→    description: str,
    93→    input_schema: type | dict[str, Any],
    94→    annotations: ToolAnnotations | None = None,
    95→) -> Callable[[Callable[[Any], Awaitable[dict[str, Any]]]], SdkMcpTool[Any]]:
    96→    """Decorator for defining MCP tools with type safety.
    97→
    98→    Creates a tool that can be used with SDK MCP servers. The tool runs
    99→    in-process within your Python application, providing better performance
   100→    than external MCP servers.
   101→
   102→    Args:
   103→        name: Unique identifier for the tool. This is what Claude will use
   104→            to reference the tool in function calls.
   105→        description: Human-readable description of what the tool does.
   106→            This helps Claude understand when to use the tool.
   107→        input_schema: Schema defining the tool's input parameters.
   108→            Can be either:
   109→            - A dictionary mapping parameter names to types (e.g., {"text": str})
   110→            - A TypedDict class for more complex schemas
   111→            - A JSON Schema dictionary for full validation
   112→
   113→    Returns:
   114→        A decorator function that wraps the tool implementation and returns
   115→        an SdkMcpTool instance ready for use with create_sdk_mcp_server().
   116→
   117→    Example:
   118→        Basic tool with simple schema:
   119→        >>> @tool("greet", "Greet a user", {"name": str})
   120→        ... async def greet(args):
   121→        ...     return {"content": [{"type": "text", "text": f"Hello, {args['name']}!"}]}
   122→
   123→        Tool with multiple parameters:
   124→        >>> @tool("add", "Add two numbers", {"a": float, "b": float})
   125→        ... async def add_numbers(args):
   126→        ...     result = args["a"] + args["b"]
   127→        ...     return {"content": [{"type": "text", "text": f"Result: {result}"}]}
   128→
   129→        Tool with error handling:
   130→        >>> @tool("divide", "Divide two numbers", {"a": float, "b": float})
   131→        ... async def divide(args):
   132→        ...     if args["b"] == 0:
   133→        ...         return {"content": [{"type": "text", "text": "Error: Division by zero"}], "is_error": True}
   134→        ...     return {"content": [{"type": "text", "text": f"Result: {args['a'] / args['b']}"}]}
   135→
   136→    Notes:
   137→        - The tool function must be async (defined with async def)
   138→        - The function receives a single dict argument with the input parameters
   139→        - The function should return a dict with a "content" key containing the response
   140→        - Errors can be indicated by including "is_error": True in the response
   141→    """
   142→
   143→    def decorator(
   144→        handler: Callable[[Any], Awaitable[dict[str, Any]]],
   145→    ) -> SdkMcpTool[Any]:
   146→        return SdkMcpTool(
   147→            name=name,
   148→            description=description,
   149→            input_schema=input_schema,
   150→            handler=handler,
   151→            annotations=annotations,
   152→        )
   153→
   154→    return decorator
   155→
   156→
   157→def create_sdk_mcp_server(
   158→    name: str, version: str = "1.0.0", tools: list[SdkMcpTool[Any]] | None = None
   159→) -> McpSdkServerConfig:
   160→    """Create an in-process MCP server that runs within your Python application.
   161→
   162→    Unlike external MCP servers that run as separate processes, SDK MCP servers
   163→    run directly in your application's process. This provides:
   164→    - Better performance (no IPC overhead)
   165→    - Simpler deployment (single process)
   166→    - Easier debugging (same process)
   167→    - Direct access to your application's state
   168→
   169→    Args:
   170→        name: Unique identifier for the server. This name is used to reference
   171→            the server in the mcp_servers configuration.
   172→        version: Server version string. Defaults to "1.0.0". This is for
   173→            informational purposes and doesn't affect functionality.
   174→        tools: List of SdkMcpTool instances created with the @tool decorator.
   175→            These are the functions that Claude can call through this server.
   176→            If None or empty, the server will have no tools (rarely useful).
   177→
   178→    Returns:
   179→        McpSdkServerConfig: A configuration object that can be passed to
   180→        ClaudeAgentOptions.mcp_servers. This config contains the server
   181→        instance and metadata needed for the SDK to route tool calls.
   182→
   183→    Example:
   184→        Simple calculator server:
   185→        >>> @tool("add", "Add numbers", {"a": float, "b": float})
   186→        ... async def add(args):
   187→        ...     return {"content": [{"type": "text", "text": f"Sum: {args['a'] + args['b']}"}]}
   188→        >>>
   189→        >>> @tool("multiply", "Multiply numbers", {"a": float, "b": float})
   190→        ... async def multiply(args):
   191→        ...     return {"content": [{"type": "text", "text": f"Product: {args['a'] * args['b']}"}]}
   192→        >>>
   193→        >>> calculator = create_sdk_mcp_server(
   194→        ...     name="calculator",
   195→        ...     version="2.0.0",
   196→        ...     tools=[add, multiply]
   197→        ... )
   198→        >>>
   199→        >>> # Use with Claude
   200→        >>> options = ClaudeAgentOptions(
   201→        ...     mcp_servers={"calc": calculator},
   202→        ...     allowed_tools=["add", "multiply"]
   203→        ... )
   204→
   205→        Server with application state access:
   206→        >>> class DataStore:
   207→        ...     def __init__(self):
   208→        ...         self.items = []
   209→        ...
   210→        >>> store = DataStore()
   211→        >>>
   212→        >>> @tool("add_item", "Add item to store", {"item": str})
   213→        ... async def add_item(args):
   214→        ...     store.items.append(args["item"])
   215→        ...     return {"content": [{"type": "text", "text": f"Added: {args['item']}"}]}
   216→        >>>
   217→        >>> server = create_sdk_mcp_server("store", tools=[add_item])
   218→
   219→    Notes:
   220→        - The server runs in the same process as your Python application
   221→        - Tools have direct access to your application's variables and state
   222→        - No subprocess or IPC overhead for tool calls
   223→        - Server lifecycle is managed automatically by the SDK
   224→
   225→    See Also:
   226→        - tool(): Decorator for creating tool functions
   227→        - ClaudeAgentOptions: Configuration for using servers with query()
   228→    """
   229→    from mcp.server import Server
   230→    from mcp.types import ImageContent, TextContent, Tool
   231→
   232→    # Create MCP server instance
   233→    server = Server(name, version=version)
   234→
   235→    # Register tools if provided
   236→    if tools:
   237→        # Store tools for access in handlers
   238→        tool_map = {tool_def.name: tool_def for tool_def in tools}
   239→
   240→        # Register list_tools handler to expose available tools
   241→        @server.list_tools()  # type: ignore[no-untyped-call,untyped-decorator]
   242→        async def list_tools() -> list[Tool]:
   243→            """Return the list of available tools."""
   244→            tool_list = []
   245→            for tool_def in tools:
   246→                # Convert input_schema to JSON Schema format
   247→                if isinstance(tool_def.input_schema, dict):
   248→                    # Check if it's already a JSON schema
   249→                    if (
   250→                        "type" in tool_def.input_schema
   251→                        and "properties" in tool_def.input_schema
   252→                    ):
   253→                        schema = tool_def.input_schema
   254→                    else:
   255→                        # Simple dict mapping names to types - convert to JSON schema
   256→                        properties = {}
   257→                        for param_name, param_type in tool_def.input_schema.items():
   258→                            if param_type is str:
   259→                                properties[param_name] = {"type": "string"}
   260→                            elif param_type is int:
   261→                                properties[param_name] = {"type": "integer"}
   262→                            elif param_type is float:
   263→                                properties[param_name] = {"type": "number"}
   264→                            elif param_type is bool:
   265→                                properties[param_name] = {"type": "boolean"}
   266→                            else:
   267→                                properties[param_name] = {"type": "string"}  # Default
   268→                        schema = {
   269→                            "type": "object",
   270→                            "properties": properties,
   271→                            "required": list(properties.keys()),
   272→                        }
   273→                else:
   274→                    # For TypedDict or other types, create basic schema
   275→                    schema = {"type": "object", "properties": {}}
   276→
   277→                tool_list.append(
   278→                    Tool(
   279→                        name=tool_def.name,
   280→                        description=tool_def.description,
   281→                        inputSchema=schema,
   282→                        annotations=tool_def.annotations,
   283→                    )
   284→                )
   285→            return tool_list
   286→
   287→        # Register call_tool handler to execute tools
   288→        @server.call_tool()  # type: ignore[untyped-decorator]
   289→        async def call_tool(name: str, arguments: dict[str, Any]) -> Any:
   290→            """Execute a tool by name with given arguments."""
   291→            if name not in tool_map:
   292→                raise ValueError(f"Tool '{name}' not found")
   293→
   294→            tool_def = tool_map[name]
   295→            # Call the tool's handler with arguments
   296→            result = await tool_def.handler(arguments)
   297→
   298→            # Convert result to MCP format
   299→            # The decorator expects us to return the content, not a CallToolResult
   300→            # It will wrap our return value in CallToolResult
   301→            content: list[TextContent | ImageContent] = []
   302→            if "content" in result:
   303→                for item in result["content"]:
   304→                    if item.get("type") == "text":
   305→                        content.append(TextContent(type="text", text=item["text"]))
   306→                    if item.get("type") == "image":
   307→                        content.append(
   308→                            ImageContent(
   309→                                type="image",
   310→                                data=item["data"],
   311→                                mimeType=item["mimeType"],
   312→                            )
   313→                        )
   314→
   315→            # Return just the content list - the decorator wraps it
   316→            return content
   317→
   318→    # Return SDK server configuration
   319→    return McpSdkServerConfig(type="sdk", name=name, instance=server)
   320→
   321→
   322→__all__ = [
   323→    # Main exports
   324→    "query",
   325→    "__version__",
   326→    # Transport
   327→    "Transport",
   328→    "ClaudeSDKClient",
   329→    # Types
   330→    "PermissionMode",
   331→    "McpServerConfig",
   332→    "McpSdkServerConfig",
   333→    "UserMessage",
   334→    "AssistantMessage",
   335→    "SystemMessage",
   336→    "ResultMessage",
   337→    "Message",
   338→    "ClaudeAgentOptions",
   339→    "TextBlock",
   340→    "ThinkingBlock",
   341→    "ThinkingConfig",
   342→    "ThinkingConfigAdaptive",
   343→    "ThinkingConfigEnabled",
   344→    "ThinkingConfigDisabled",
   345→    "ToolUseBlock",
   346→    "ToolResultBlock",
   347→    "ContentBlock",
   348→    # Tool callbacks
   349→    "CanUseTool",
   350→    "ToolPermissionContext",
   351→    "PermissionResult",
   352→    "PermissionResultAllow",
   353→    "PermissionResultDeny",
   354→    "PermissionUpdate",
   355→    # Hook support
   356→    "HookCallback",
   357→    "HookContext",
   358→    "HookInput",
   359→    "BaseHookInput",
   360→    "PreToolUseHookInput",
   361→    "PostToolUseHookInput",
   362→    "PostToolUseFailureHookInput",
   363→    "PostToolUseFailureHookSpecificOutput",
   364→    "UserPromptSubmitHookInput",

   376→    # Agent support

   379→    # Plugin support

   383→    # Sandbox support
```

> AGENT

**Root cause found!** DuckDB's `conn.execute()` returns `None` for empty strings, semicolons-only, whitespace-only, and comment-only SQL. When the Claude agent generates an empty or comment-only SQL (which can happen with non-SQL questions), `result` is `None`, and `result.description` throws `'NoneType' object has no attribute 'description'`.

Phase 2 confirms the pattern. Phase 3: the fix is to add a guard in `database.py` for `result` being `None`.

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01HUantaN5h413t3PQSCNrRe
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/database.py",
  "old_string": "    def execute_query(self, sql: str) -> dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Execute a SQL query and return results as dict with columns, rows, rowCount.\"\"\"\n        result = self.conn.execute(sql)\n        columns = [desc[0] for desc in result.description] if result.description else []\n        rows = [dict(zip(columns, row)) for row in result.fetchall()] if columns else []\n        return {\"columns\": columns, \"rows\": rows, \"rowCount\": len(rows)}",
  "new_string": "    def execute_query(self, sql: str) -> dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Execute a SQL query and return results as dict with columns, rows, rowCount.\"\"\"\n        result = self.conn.execute(sql)\n        if result is None:\n            return {\"columns\": [], \"rows\": [], \"rowCount\": 0}\n        columns = [desc[0] for desc in result.description] if result.description else []\n        rows = [dict(zip(columns, row)) for row in result.fetchall()] if columns else []\n        return {\"columns\": columns, \"rows\": rows, \"rowCount\": len(rows)}"
}
```

```
     1→import duckdb
     2→import os
     3→import tempfile
     4→from typing import Any
     5→
     6→
     7→class Database:
     8→    def __init__(self):
     9→        self.conn = duckdb.connect(":memory:")
    10→
    11→    def execute_query(self, sql: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    12→        """Execute a SQL query and return results as dict with columns, rows, rowCount."""
    13→        result = self.conn.execute(sql)
    14→        if result is None:
    15→            return {"columns": [], "rows": [], "rowCount": 0}
    16→        columns = [desc[0] for desc in result.description] if result.description else []
    17→        rows = [dict(zip(columns, row)) for row in result.fetchall()] if columns else []
    18→        return {"columns": columns, "rows": rows, "rowCount": len(rows)}
    19→
    20→    def load_csv(self, file_bytes: bytes, filename: str, table_name: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    21→        """Load a CSV file into a DuckDB table. Returns table info."""
    22→        with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(suffix=".csv", delete=False) as tmp:
    23→            tmp.write(file_bytes)
    24→            tmp_path = tmp.name
    25→        try:
    26→            self.conn.execute(
    27→                f'CREATE OR REPLACE TABLE "{table_name}" AS SELECT * FROM read_csv_auto(\'{tmp_path}\')'
    28→            )
    29→        finally:
    30→            os.unlink(tmp_path)
    31→        return self.get_table_info(table_name)
    32→
    33→    def get_table_info(self, table_name: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    34→        """Get info about a specific table."""
    35→        cols_result = self.conn.execute(f'DESCRIBE "{table_name}"')
    36→        columns = [
    37→            {"name": row[0], "type": row[1]}
    38→            for row in cols_result.fetchall()
    39→        ]
    40→        count_result = self.conn.execute(f'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM "{table_name}"')
    41→        row_count = count_result.fetchone()[0]
    42→        return {"name": table_name, "columns": columns, "rowCount": row_count}
    43→
    44→    def list_tables(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    45→        """List all tables with their schema info."""
    46→        tables_result = self.conn.execute("SHOW TABLES")
    47→        table_names = [row[0] for row in tables_result.fetchall()]
    48→        return [self.get_table_info(name) for name in table_names]
    49→
    50→    def drop_table(self, table_name: str) -> None:
    51→        """Drop a table."""
    52→        self.conn.execute(f'DROP TABLE IF EXISTS "{table_name}"')
    53→
    54→    def load_sample_data(self, csv_path: str, table_name: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    55→        """Load sample CSV from a file path."""
    56→        self.conn.execute(
    57→            f'CREATE OR REPLACE TABLE "{table_name}" AS SELECT * FROM read_csv_auto(\'{csv_path}\')'
    58→        )
    59→        return self.get_table_info(table_name)
    60→
    61→
    62→# Singleton instance
    63→db = Database()
    64→
```

> AGENT

All tests pass. Here's a summary of the debugging:

## Root Cause

**`database.py:13`** — DuckDB's `conn.execute(sql)` returns `None` for empty/whitespace/comment-only SQL strings (e.g., `""`, `";"`, `"  "`, `"-- comment"`). The code guarded against `result.description` being `None`, but not `result` itself being `None`.

When the agent receives a non-SQL question like "what is your OS and CPU, memory info", it sometimes generates an empty or trivial SQL string. This causes `conn.execute()` to return `None`, and `result.description` raises `'NoneType' object has no attribute 'description'`. The error is caught and displayed inside the thinking block's `InlineQueryResult` component.

## Fix

Added a `None` guard in `database.py:14-15`:

```python
result = self.conn.execute(sql)
if result is None:
    return {"columns": [], "rows": [], "rowCount": 0}
```

This gracefully returns an empty result set instead of crashing.

```
     1→import json
     2→import logging
     3→from typing import AsyncIterator
     4→
     5→from claude_agent_sdk import (
     6→    ClaudeSDKClient,
     7→    ClaudeAgentOptions,
     8→    AssistantMessage,
     9→    ResultMessage,
    10→    ToolUseBlock,
    11→)
    12→from claude_agent_sdk._errors import MessageParseError
    13→from claude_agent_sdk._internal.message_parser import parse_message
    14→from claude_agent_sdk.types import StreamEvent
    15→from app.tools import create_duckdb_server
    16→from app.database import db
    17→
    18→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    19→
    20→
    21→def build_system_prompt() -> str:
    22→    tables = db.list_tables()
    23→    prompt = """You are a helpful data analyst assistant working with a DuckDB database.
    24→You can execute SQL queries using the execute_sql tool to answer questions about the user's data.
    25→
    26→Guidelines:
    27→- Write clear, efficient DuckDB SQL queries
    28→- When exploring data, start with small queries (use LIMIT)
    29→- Explain your findings in plain language after getting results
    30→- If a query fails, try to fix it and retry
    31→- Use double quotes for table and column names that might conflict with reserved words
    32→"""
    33→    if not tables:
    34→        prompt += "\nNo tables are currently loaded. Ask the user to upload a CSV file first."
    35→    else:
    36→        prompt += "\nCurrently loaded tables:\n"
    37→        for table in tables:
    38→            prompt += f'\nTable: "{table["name"]}" ({table["rowCount"]} rows)\nColumns:\n'
    39→            for col in table["columns"]:
    40→                prompt += f'  - "{col["name"]}" ({col["type"]})\n'
    41→    return prompt
    42→
    43→
    44→async def stream_chat(message: str, session_id: str | None = None) -> AsyncIterator[str]:
    45→    """Stream agent chat responses as SSE events."""
    46→    duckdb_server = create_duckdb_server()
    47→
    48→    options = ClaudeAgentOptions(
    49→        system_prompt=build_system_prompt(),
    50→        mcp_servers={"duckdb": duckdb_server},
    51→        allowed_tools=["mcp__duckdb__execute_sql"],
    52→        permission_mode="bypassPermissions",
    53→        max_turns=20,
    54→        include_partial_messages=True,
    55→    )
    56→
    57→    client = ClaudeSDKClient(options=options)
    58→    actual_session_id = session_id
    59→
    60→    try:
    61→        await client.connect()
    62→        await client.query(message, session_id=session_id or "default")
    63→
    64→        current_text = ""
    65→        has_tool_calls = False
    66→        thinking_sent = False
    67→
    68→        async for raw_data in client._query.receive_messages():
    69→            try:
    70→                msg = parse_message(raw_data)
    71→            except MessageParseError as e:
    72→                logger.debug("Skipping unrecognized message: %s", e)
    73→                continue
    74→
    75→            if isinstance(msg, StreamEvent):
    76→                event = msg.event
    77→                if not actual_session_id:
    78→                    actual_session_id = msg.session_id
    79→
    80→                event_type = event.get("type", "")
    81→
    82→                if event_type == "content_block_delta":
    83→                    delta = event.get("delta", {})
    84→                    delta_type = delta.get("type", "")
    85→                    if delta_type == "text_delta":
    86→                        text = delta.get("text", "")
    87→                        current_text += text
    88→                        event_name = "thinking" if not has_tool_calls else "answer"
    89→                        yield f"event: {event_name}\ndata: {json.dumps({'text': text})}\n\n"
    90→
    91→                elif event_type == "content_block_start":
    92→                    block = event.get("content_block", {})
    93→                    if block.get("type") == "tool_use":
    94→                        if current_text.strip() and not thinking_sent:
    95→                            thinking_sent = True
    96→                        has_tool_calls = True
    97→
    98→            elif isinstance(msg, AssistantMessage):
    99→                if not actual_session_id:
   100→                    actual_session_id = "default"
   101→
   102→                for block in msg.content:
   103→                    if isinstance(block, ToolUseBlock):
   104→                        sql = block.input.get("sql", "")
   105→                        yield f"event: tool_call\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': block.id, 'sql': sql})}\n\n"
   106→                        has_tool_calls = True
   107→
   108→                        # Execute the query directly for the UI since
   109→                        # ToolResultBlock is not exposed in the SDK stream
   110→                        try:
   111→                            result = db.execute_query(sql)
   112→                            truncated = result["rows"][:100]
   113→                            yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': block.id, 'sql': sql, 'columns': result['columns'], 'rows': truncated, 'rowCount': result['rowCount']}, default=str)}\n\n"
   114→                        except Exception as e:
   115→                            yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': block.id, 'sql': sql, 'error': str(e)})}\n\n"
   116→
   117→            elif isinstance(msg, ResultMessage):
   118→                actual_session_id = msg.session_id
   119→                if msg.is_error and msg.result:
   120→                    yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': msg.result})}\n\n"
   121→                yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
   122→                break
   123→
   124→    except Exception as e:
   125→        yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': str(e)})}\n\n"
   126→    finally:
   127→        try:
   128→            await client.disconnect()
   129→        except Exception:
   130→            pass
   131→
```

```
     4→  onTextChunk: (text: string) => void;
     5→  onToolCall: (toolCallId: string, sql: string) => void;
     6→  onToolResult: (result: ToolCallResult) => void;
     7→  onDone: (sessionId: string | null) => void;
     8→  onError: (error: string) => void;
     9→}
    10→
    11→export async function runAgentLoop(
    12→  message: string,
    13→  sessionId: string | null,
    14→  callbacks: AgentCallbacks,
    15→  signal?: AbortSignal,
    16→): Promise<void> {
    17→  try {
    18→    const response = await fetch('/api/chat', {
    19→      method: 'POST',
    20→      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    21→      body: JSON.stringify({ message, session_id: sessionId }),
    22→      signal,
    23→    });
    24→
    25→    if (!response.ok) {
    26→      const errorText = await response.text();
    27→      callbacks.onError(`Server error: ${errorText}`);
    28→      return;
    29→    }
    30→
    31→    const reader = response.body?.getReader();
    32→    if (!reader) {
    33→      callbacks.onError('No response stream');
    34→      return;
    35→    }
    36→
    37→    const decoder = new TextDecoder();
    38→    let buffer = '';
    39→
    40→    while (true) {
    41→      const { done, value } = await reader.read();
    42→      if (done) break;
    43→
    44→      buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
    45→      const lines = buffer.split('\n');
    46→      buffer = lines.pop() ?? '';
    47→
    48→      let eventType = '';
    49→      for (const line of lines) {
    50→        if (line.startsWith('event: ')) {
    51→          eventType = line.slice(7).trim();
    52→        } else if (line.startsWith('data: ')) {
    53→          const data = line.slice(6);
    54→          try {
    55→            const parsed = JSON.parse(data);
    56→            handleSSEEvent(eventType, parsed, callbacks);
    57→          } catch {
    58→            // Skip malformed JSON
    59→          }
    60→          eventType = '';
    61→        }
    62→      }
    63→    }
    64→  } catch (e: unknown) {
    65→    if (signal?.aborted) return;
    66→    const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Connection failed';
    67→    callbacks.onError(msg);
    68→  }
    69→}
    70→
    71→function handleSSEEvent(
    72→  eventType: string,
    73→  data: Record<string, unknown>,
    74→  callbacks: AgentCallbacks,
    75→) {
    76→  switch (eventType) {
    77→    case 'thinking':
    78→    case 'answer':
    79→      callbacks.onTextChunk(data.text as string);
    80→      break;
    81→    case 'tool_call':
    82→      callbacks.onToolCall(data.id as string, data.sql as string);
    83→      break;
    84→    case 'tool_result': {
    85→      const result: ToolCallResult = {
    86→        toolCallId: (data.id as string) ?? '',
    87→        sql: (data.sql as string) ?? '',
    88→        columns: (data.columns as string[]) ?? [],
    89→        rows: (data.rows as Record<string, unknown>[]) ?? [],
    90→        rowCount: (data.rowCount as number) ?? 0,
    91→        error: (data.error as string) ?? undefined,
    92→        rawContent: (data.content as string) ?? undefined,
    93→      };
    94→      callbacks.onToolResult(result);
    95→      break;
    96→    }
    97→    case 'done':
    98→      callbacks.onDone((data.session_id as string) ?? null);
    99→      break;
   100→    case 'error':
   101→      callbacks.onError((data.message as string) ?? 'Unknown error');
   102→      break;
   103→  }
   104→}
   105→
```

```
    10→import type { ChatMessage, ContentSegment, TableInfo, ToolCallResult } from './types';
    11→

    18→

    25→
    26→function generateId() {
    27→  return Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10);

    29→
    30→export function AgentProvider({
    31→  children,
    32→  refreshTables,
    33→}: {
    34→  children: ReactNode;
    35→  tables: TableInfo[];
    36→  refreshTables: () => Promise<void>;
    37→}) {
    38→  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<ChatMessage[]>([]);
    39→  const [isStreaming, setIsStreaming] = useState(false);

    41→  const textBufferRef = useRef('');

    43→  const assistantIdRef = useRef('');

    45→  const currentTextRef = useRef('');
    46→  const sessionIdRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
    47→
    48→  const flushText = useCallback(() => {
    49→    const text = textBufferRef.current;
    50→    if (!text) return;
    51→    const id = assistantIdRef.current;
    52→    currentTextRef.current += text;
    53→    setMessages((prev) =>
    54→      prev.map((m) =>
    55→        m.id === id ? { ...m, content: m.content + text } : m
    56→      )
    57→    );
    58→    textBufferRef.current = '';
    59→  }, []);
    60→
    61→  const sendMessage = useCallback(
    62→    async (text: string) => {
    63→      if (isStreaming) return;
    64→
    65→      const userMsg: ChatMessage = {
    66→        id: generateId(),
    67→        role: 'user',
    68→        content: text,
    69→      };
    70→
    71→      const assistantId = generateId();
    72→      assistantIdRef.current = assistantId;
    73→      const assistantMsg: ChatMessage = {
    74→        id: assistantId,
    75→        role: 'assistant',
    76→        content: '',
    77→        toolCalls: [],
    78→        isStreaming: true,
    79→      };
    80→
    81→      setMessages((prev) => [...prev, userMsg, assistantMsg]);
    82→      setIsStreaming(true);
    83→      textBufferRef.current = '';
    84→      segmentsRef.current = [];
    85→      currentTextRef.current = '';
    86→
    87→      const controller = new AbortController();
    88→      abortRef.current = controller;
    89→
    90→      await runAgentLoop(
    91→        text,
    92→        sessionIdRef.current,
    93→        {
    94→          onTextChunk: (chunk) => {
    95→            textBufferRef.current += chunk;
    96→            if (!flushTimerRef.current) {
    97→              flushTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
    98→                flushText();
    99→                flushTimerRef.current = null;
   100→              }, 50);
   101→            }
   102→          },
   103→          onToolCall: (toolCallId: string, sql: string) => {
   104→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   105→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   106→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   107→            }
   108→            flushText();
   109→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   110→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'thinking', text: currentTextRef.current });
   111→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   112→            }
   113→            // Add a pending tool segment so the SQL is shown immediately
   114→            segmentsRef.current.push({
   115→              type: 'tool',
   116→              toolResult: { toolCallId, sql, columns: [], rows: [], rowCount: 0 },
   117→            });
   118→            setMessages((prev) =>
   119→              prev.map((m) =>
   120→                m.id === assistantId
   121→                  ? { ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   122→                  : m
   123→              )
   124→            );
   125→          },
   126→          onToolResult: (result: ToolCallResult) => {
   127→            // Replace the pending tool segment with the full result
   128→            const pendingIdx = segmentsRef.current.findIndex(
   129→              (s) => s.type === 'tool' && s.toolResult?.toolCallId === result.toolCallId
   130→            );
   131→            if (pendingIdx !== -1) {
   132→              segmentsRef.current[pendingIdx] = { type: 'tool', toolResult: result };
   133→            } else {
   134→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'tool', toolResult: result });
   135→            }
   136→            setMessages((prev) =>
   137→              prev.map((m) =>
   138→                m.id === assistantId
   139→                  ? { ...m, toolCalls: [...(m.toolCalls || []), result], segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   140→                  : m
   141→              )
   142→            );
   143→            refreshTables();
   144→          },
   145→          onDone: (newSessionId) => {
   146→            if (newSessionId) sessionIdRef.current = newSessionId;
   147→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   148→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   149→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   150→            }
   151→            flushText();
   152→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   153→              segmentsRef.current.push({
   154→                type: 'answer',
   155→                text: currentTextRef.current,
   156→              });
   157→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   158→            }
   159→            setMessages((prev) =>
   160→              prev.map((m) =>
   161→                m.id === assistantId
   162→                  ? { ...m, isStreaming: false, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   163→                  : m
   164→              )
   165→            );
   166→            setIsStreaming(false);
   167→            abortRef.current = null;
   168→          },
   169→          onError: (error) => {
   170→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   171→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   172→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   173→            }
   174→            flushText();
   175→            setMessages((prev) =>
   176→              prev.map((m) =>
   177→                m.id === assistantId
   178→                  ? { ...m, content: m.content + `\n\n**Error:** ${error}`, isStreaming: false }
   179→                  : m
   180→              )
   181→            );
   182→            setIsStreaming(false);
   183→            abortRef.current = null;
   184→          },
   185→        },
   186→        controller.signal
   187→      );
   188→    },
   189→    [isStreaming, messages, flushText, refreshTables]
   190→  );
   191→
   192→  const clearMessages = useCallback(() => {
   193→    if (abortRef.current) {
   194→      abortRef.current.abort();
   195→    }
   196→    setMessages([]);
   197→    setIsStreaming(false);
   198→    sessionIdRef.current = null;
   199→  }, []);
   200→
   201→  return (
   202→    <AgentContext.Provider
   203→      value={{ messages, isStreaming, sendMessage, clearMessages }}
   204→    >
   205→      {children}
   206→    </AgentContext.Provider>
   207→  );
   208→}
   209→
   210→export function useAgent() {
   211→  return useContext(AgentContext);
   212→}
   213→
```
