     1→import ReactMarkdown from 'react-markdown';
     2→import remarkGfm from 'remark-gfm';
     3→import type { ChatMessage, ContentSegment } from '../types';
     4→import { InlineQueryResult } from './InlineQueryResult';
     5→import './MessageBubble.css';
     6→
     7→function getLastThinkingLine(segments: ContentSegment[], streamingRemainder?: string): string {
     8→  // Use streaming remainder if available
     9→  if (streamingRemainder?.trim()) {
    10→    const lines = streamingRemainder.trim().split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim());
    11→    const last = lines[lines.length - 1] || '';
    12→    return last.length > 100 ? last.slice(0, 100) + '...' : last;
    13→  }
    14→  // Otherwise use last thinking segment's last line
    15→  for (let i = segments.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    16→    if (segments[i].type === 'thinking' && segments[i].text?.trim()) {
    17→      const lines = segments[i].text!.trim().split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim());
    18→      const last = lines[lines.length - 1] || '';
    19→      return last.length > 100 ? last.slice(0, 100) + '...' : last;
    20→    }
    21→  }
    22→  return 'Thinking...';
    23→}
    24→
    25→function ThinkingBlock({ segments, streamingRemainder, isActivelyStreaming }: {
    26→  segments: ContentSegment[];
    27→  streamingRemainder?: string;
    28→  isActivelyStreaming: boolean;
    29→}) {
    30→  // All non-answer segments go inside the thinking block
    31→  const thinkingSegments = segments.filter((s) => s.type !== 'answer');
    32→  const hasContent = thinkingSegments.some(
    33→    (s) => (s.type === 'thinking' && s.text?.trim()) || (s.type === 'tool' && s.toolResult)
    34→  ) || streamingRemainder?.trim();
    35→
    36→  if (!hasContent) return null;
    37→
    38→  const summary = getLastThinkingLine(segments, streamingRemainder);
    39→
    40→  return (
    41→    <details className="message-bubble__segment message-bubble__segment--thinking message-bubble__collapsible" open={isActivelyStreaming || undefined}>
    42→      <summary className="message-bubble__collapsible-summary">
    43→        <span className="message-bubble__segment-label">Thinking</span>
    44→        <span className="message-bubble__collapsible-preview">{summary}</span>
    45→      </summary>
    46→      <div className="message-bubble__thinking-body">
    47→        {thinkingSegments.map((seg, i) => {
    48→          if (seg.type === 'thinking' && seg.text?.trim()) {
    49→            return (
    50→              <div key={i} className="message-bubble__segment-content">
    51→                <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}>{seg.text}</ReactMarkdown>
    52→              </div>
    53→            );
    54→          }
    55→          if (seg.type === 'tool' && seg.toolResult) {
    56→            return (
    57→              <div key={i} className="message-bubble__tool-segment">
    58→                <InlineQueryResult result={seg.toolResult} />
    59→              </div>
    60→            );
    61→          }
    62→          return null;
    63→        })}
    64→        {streamingRemainder?.trim() && (
    65→          <div className="message-bubble__segment-content">
    66→            <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}>{streamingRemainder}</ReactMarkdown>
    67→          </div>
    68→        )}
    69→      </div>
    70→    </details>
    71→  );
    72→}
    73→
    74→export function MessageBubble({ message }: { message: ChatMessage }) {
    75→  const isUser = message.role === 'user';
    76→  const hasSegments = !isUser && message.segments && message.segments.length > 0;
    77→
    78→  // Compute streaming remainder text
    79→  let streamingRemainder: string | undefined;
    80→  if (hasSegments && message.isStreaming && message.content) {
    81→    const segmentedText = message.segments!
    82→      .filter((s) => s.type !== 'tool')
    83→      .map((s) => s.text || '')
    84→      .join('');
    85→    const remaining = message.content.slice(segmentedText.length);
    86→    if (remaining.trim()) {
    87→      streamingRemainder = remaining;
    88→    }
    89→  }
    90→
    91→  // Check if currently in a thinking-only phase (no answer segment yet)
    92→  const hasAnswer = hasSegments && message.segments!.some((s) => s.type === 'answer');
    93→  const isThinkingPhase = !!message.isStreaming && !hasAnswer;
    94→
    95→  // Extract answer segments
    96→  const answerSegments = hasSegments
    97→    ? message.segments!.filter((s) => s.type === 'answer' && s.text?.trim())
    98→    : [];
    99→
   100→  return (
   101→    <div className={`message-bubble message-bubble--${message.role}`}>
   102→      <div className="message-bubble__header">
   103→        {isUser ? 'You' : 'Assistant'}
   104→      </div>
   105→
   106→      {hasSegments ? (
   107→        <div className="message-bubble__segments">
   108→          <ThinkingBlock
   109→            segments={message.segments!}
   110→            streamingRemainder={isThinkingPhase ? streamingRemainder : undefined}
   111→            isActivelyStreaming={isThinkingPhase}
   112→          />
   113→          {answerSegments.map((seg, i) => (
   114→            <div key={i} className="message-bubble__segment message-bubble__segment--answer">
   115→              <div className="message-bubble__segment-label message-bubble__segment-label--answer">Answer</div>
   116→              <div className="message-bubble__segment-content">
   117→                <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}>{seg.text!}</ReactMarkdown>
   118→              </div>
   119→            </div>
   120→          ))}
   121→          {message.isStreaming && !message.content && (
   122→            <span className="message-bubble__typing">Thinking...</span>
   123→          )}
   124→        </div>
   125→      ) : (
   126→        <>
   127→          <div className="message-bubble__content">
   128→            {message.content ? (
   129→              <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}>{message.content}</ReactMarkdown>
   130→            ) : message.isStreaming ? (
   131→              <span className="message-bubble__typing">Thinking...</span>
   132→            ) : null}
   133→          </div>
   134→          {message.toolCalls && message.toolCalls.length > 0 && (
   135→            <div className="message-bubble__tools">
   136→              {message.toolCalls.map((tc) => (
   137→                <InlineQueryResult key={tc.toolCallId} result={tc} />
   138→              ))}
   139→            </div>
   140→          )}
   141→        </>
   142→      )}
   143→    </div>
   144→  );
   145→}
   146→

Here's a summary of all changes made:

## Changes Summary

### Bug fix: `'NoneType' object has no attribute 'description'`
**`backend/app/database.py`** - Added `None` guard for `conn.execute()` which returns `None` for empty/comment-only SQL.

### Feature: Show thinking steps for non-SQL questions

**4 files changed across backend and frontend:**

1. **`backend/app/agent.py`** - Now handles `thinking_delta` events from Claude's extended thinking (previously completely ignored). Thinking content is sent as `"thinking"` SSE events, and the text response after thinking is sent as `"answer"` events.

2. **`frontend/src/agent/agentService.ts`** - Split the single `onTextChunk` callback into `onThinkingChunk` and `onAnswerChunk` to distinguish between phases.

3. **`frontend/src/AgentContext.tsx`** - Tracks the current phase (`thinking` vs `answer`). When the first answer chunk arrives, it flushes accumulated thinking text into a thinking segment, then starts accumulating answer text separately.

4. **`frontend/src/components/MessageBubble.tsx`** - Uses the `currentPhase` field to correctly determine when thinking is done. Shows streaming answer text in the answer area (not the thinking block) during the answer phase.

### How it works now

| Scenario | Before | After |
|---|---|---|
| Non-SQL question (e.g., "what is your OS") | No thinking block shown, all text in answer | Thinking block shows Claude's reasoning, answer shown separately |
| SQL question with tool calls | Thinking + tool results + answer | Same behavior (unchanged) |

     1→import json
     2→import logging
     3→from typing import AsyncIterator
     4→
     5→from claude_agent_sdk import (
     6→    ClaudeSDKClient,
     7→    ClaudeAgentOptions,
     8→    AssistantMessage,
     9→    ResultMessage,
    10→    ToolUseBlock,
    11→)
    12→from claude_agent_sdk._errors import MessageParseError
    13→from claude_agent_sdk._internal.message_parser import parse_message
    14→from claude_agent_sdk.types import StreamEvent
    15→from app.tools import create_duckdb_server
    16→from app.database import db
    17→
    18→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    19→
    20→
    21→def build_system_prompt() -> str:
    22→    tables = db.list_tables()
    23→    prompt = """You are a helpful data analyst assistant working with a DuckDB database.
    24→You can execute SQL queries using the execute_sql tool to answer questions about the user's data.
    25→
    26→Guidelines:
    27→- Write clear, efficient DuckDB SQL queries
    28→- When exploring data, start with small queries (use LIMIT)
    29→- Explain your findings in plain language after getting results
    30→- If a query fails, try to fix it and retry
    31→- Use double quotes for table and column names that might conflict with reserved words
    32→"""
    33→    if not tables:
    34→        prompt += "\nNo tables are currently loaded. Ask the user to upload a CSV file first."
    35→    else:
    36→        prompt += "\nCurrently loaded tables:\n"
    37→        for table in tables:
    38→            prompt += f'\nTable: "{table["name"]}" ({table["rowCount"]} rows)\nColumns:\n'
    39→            for col in table["columns"]:
    40→                prompt += f'  - "{col["name"]}" ({col["type"]})\n'
    41→    return prompt
    42→
    43→
    44→async def stream_chat(message: str, session_id: str | None = None) -> AsyncIterator[str]:
    45→    """Stream agent chat responses as SSE events."""
    46→    duckdb_server = create_duckdb_server()
    47→
    48→    options = ClaudeAgentOptions(
    49→        system_prompt=build_system_prompt(),
    50→        mcp_servers={"duckdb": duckdb_server},
    51→        allowed_tools=["mcp__duckdb__execute_sql"],
    52→        permission_mode="bypassPermissions",
    53→        max_turns=20,
    54→        include_partial_messages=True,
    55→    )
    56→
    57→    client = ClaudeSDKClient(options=options)
    58→    actual_session_id = session_id
    59→
    60→    try:
    61→        await client.connect()
    62→        await client.query(message, session_id=session_id or "default")
    63→
    64→        current_text = ""
    65→        has_tool_calls = False
    66→        thinking_sent = False
    67→        has_thinking = False
    68→
    69→        async for raw_data in client._query.receive_messages():
    70→            try:
    71→                msg = parse_message(raw_data)
    72→            except MessageParseError as e:
    73→                logger.debug("Skipping unrecognized message: %s", e)
    74→                continue
    75→
    76→            if isinstance(msg, StreamEvent):
    77→                event = msg.event
    78→                if not actual_session_id:
    79→                    actual_session_id = msg.session_id
    80→
    81→                event_type = event.get("type", "")
    82→
    83→                if event_type == "content_block_delta":
    84→                    delta = event.get("delta", {})
    85→                    delta_type = delta.get("type", "")
    86→                    if delta_type == "thinking_delta":
    87→                        text = delta.get("thinking", "")
    88→                        if text:
    89→                            current_text += text
    90→                            yield f"event: thinking\ndata: {json.dumps({'text': text})}\n\n"
    91→                    elif delta_type == "text_delta":
    92→                        text = delta.get("text", "")
    93→                        current_text += text
    94→                        event_name = "answer" if (has_thinking or has_tool_calls) else "thinking"
    95→                        yield f"event: {event_name}\ndata: {json.dumps({'text': text})}\n\n"
    96→
    97→                elif event_type == "content_block_start":
    98→                    block = event.get("content_block", {})
    99→                    block_type = block.get("type")
   100→                    if block_type == "thinking":
   101→                        has_thinking = True
   102→                    elif block_type == "tool_use":
   103→                        if current_text.strip() and not thinking_sent:
   104→                            thinking_sent = True
   105→                        has_tool_calls = True
   106→
   107→            elif isinstance(msg, AssistantMessage):
   108→                if not actual_session_id:
   109→                    actual_session_id = "default"
   110→
   111→                for block in msg.content:
   112→                    if isinstance(block, ToolUseBlock):
   113→                        sql = block.input.get("sql", "")
   114→                        yield f"event: tool_call\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': block.id, 'sql': sql})}\n\n"
   115→                        has_tool_calls = True
   116→
   117→                        # Execute the query directly for the UI since
   118→                        # ToolResultBlock is not exposed in the SDK stream
   119→                        try:
   120→                            result = db.execute_query(sql)
   121→                            truncated = result["rows"][:100]
   122→                            yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': block.id, 'sql': sql, 'columns': result['columns'], 'rows': truncated, 'rowCount': result['rowCount']}, default=str)}\n\n"
   123→                        except Exception as e:
   124→                            yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': block.id, 'sql': sql, 'error': str(e)})}\n\n"
   125→
   126→            elif isinstance(msg, ResultMessage):
   127→                actual_session_id = msg.session_id
   128→                if msg.is_error and msg.result:
   129→                    yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': msg.result})}\n\n"
   130→                yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
   131→                break
   132→
   133→    except Exception as e:
   134→        yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': str(e)})}\n\n"
   135→    finally:

> AGENT

I see the root cause. On line 94:

```python
event_name = "answer" if (has_thinking or has_tool_calls) else "thinking"
```

When `has_thinking` is True, ALL `text_delta` gets labeled as "answer" — including pre-tool text. The frontend's `onToolCall` then creates a 'thinking' segment from what it thinks is thinking text, but it was actually answer text that gets lost. And if the agent makes tool calls and doesn't produce text AFTER them, the answer segment is empty.

**Hypothesis:** The `has_thinking` flag causes pre-tool `text_delta` to be mislabeled as "answer", breaking the frontend's segment creation flow.

**Fix:** Instead of using `has_thinking` to determine event names for `text_delta`, use a dedicated `thinking_done` event emitted when a text block starts after thinking. Keep `text_delta` labeling using only `has_tool_calls` (the original logic).

{
  "file_path": "/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/frontend/src/agent/agentService.ts",
  "content": "import type { ToolCallResult } from '../types';\n\ninterface AgentCallbacks {\n  onTextChunk: (text: string) => void;\n  onThinkingDone: () => void;\n  onToolCall: (toolCallId: string, sql: string) => void;\n  onToolResult: (result: ToolCallResult) => void;\n  onDone: (sessionId: string | null) => void;\n  onError: (error: string) => void;\n}\n\nexport type { AgentCallbacks };\n\nexport async function runAgentLoop(\n  message: string,\n  sessionId: string | null,\n  callbacks: AgentCallbacks,\n  signal?: AbortSignal,\n): Promise<void> {\n  try {\n    const response = await fetch('/api/chat', {\n      method: 'POST',\n      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n      body: JSON.stringify({ message, session_id: sessionId }),\n      signal,\n    });\n\n    if (!response.ok) {\n      const errorText = await response.text();\n      callbacks.onError(`Server error: ${errorText}`);\n      return;\n    }\n\n    const reader = response.body?.getReader();\n    if (!reader) {\n      callbacks.onError('No response stream');\n      return;\n    }\n\n    const decoder = new TextDecoder();\n    let buffer = '';\n\n    while (true) {\n      const { done, value } = await reader.read();\n      if (done) break;\n\n      buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });\n      const lines = buffer.split('\\n');\n      buffer = lines.pop() ?? '';\n\n      let eventType = '';\n      for (const line of lines) {\n        if (line.startsWith('event: ')) {\n          eventType = line.slice(7).trim();\n        } else if (line.startsWith('data: ')) {\n          const data = line.slice(6);\n          try {\n            const parsed = JSON.parse(data);\n            handleSSEEvent(eventType, parsed, callbacks);\n          } catch {\n            // Skip malformed JSON\n          }\n          eventType = '';\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  } catch (e: unknown) {\n    if (signal?.aborted) return;\n    const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Connection failed';\n    callbacks.onError(msg);\n  }\n}\n\nfunction handleSSEEvent(\n  eventType: string,\n  data: Record<string, unknown>,\n  callbacks: AgentCallbacks,\n) {\n  switch (eventType) {\n    case 'thinking':\n    case 'answer':\n      callbacks.onTextChunk(data.text as string);\n      break;\n    case 'thinking_done':\n      callbacks.onThinkingDone();\n      break;\n    case 'tool_call':\n      callbacks.onToolCall(data.id as string, data.sql as string);\n      break;\n    case 'tool_result': {\n      const result: ToolCallResult = {\n        toolCallId: (data.id as string) ?? '',\n        sql: (data.sql as string) ?? '',\n        columns: (data.columns as string[]) ?? [],\n        rows: (data.rows as Record<string, unknown>[]) ?? [],\n        rowCount: (data.rowCount as number) ?? 0,\n        error: (data.error as string) ?? undefined,\n        rawContent: (data.content as string) ?? undefined,\n      };\n      callbacks.onToolResult(result);\n      break;\n    }\n    case 'done':\n      callbacks.onDone((data.session_id as string) ?? null);\n      break;\n    case 'error':\n      callbacks.onError((data.message as string) ?? 'Unknown error');\n      break;\n  }\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/frontend/src/AgentContext.tsx",
  "content": "import {\n  createContext,\n  useCallback,\n  useContext,\n  useRef,\n  useState,\n  type ReactNode,\n} from 'react';\nimport { runAgentLoop } from './agent/agentService';\nimport type { ChatMessage, ContentSegment, TableInfo, ToolCallResult } from './types';\n\ninterface AgentContextValue {\n  messages: ChatMessage[];\n  isStreaming: boolean;\n  sendMessage: (text: string) => void;\n  clearMessages: () => void;\n}\n\nconst AgentContext = createContext<AgentContextValue>({\n  messages: [],\n  isStreaming: false,\n  sendMessage: () => {},\n  clearMessages: () => {},\n});\n\nfunction generateId() {\n  return Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10);\n}\n\nexport function AgentProvider({\n  children,\n  refreshTables,\n}: {\n  children: ReactNode;\n  tables: TableInfo[];\n  refreshTables: () => Promise<void>;\n}) {\n  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<ChatMessage[]>([]);\n  const [isStreaming, setIsStreaming] = useState(false);\n  const abortRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);\n  const textBufferRef = useRef('');\n  const flushTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);\n  const assistantIdRef = useRef('');\n  const segmentsRef = useRef<ContentSegment[]>([]);\n  const currentTextRef = useRef('');\n  const sessionIdRef = useRef<string | null>(null);\n\n  const flushText = useCallback(() => {\n    const text = textBufferRef.current;\n    if (!text) return;\n    const id = assistantIdRef.current;\n    currentTextRef.current += text;\n    setMessages((prev) =>\n      prev.map((m) =>\n        m.id === id ? { ...m, content: m.content + text } : m\n      )\n    );\n    textBufferRef.current = '';\n  }, []);\n\n  const sendMessage = useCallback(\n    async (text: string) => {\n      if (isStreaming) return;\n\n      const userMsg: ChatMessage = {\n        id: generateId(),\n        role: 'user',\n        content: text,\n      };\n\n      const assistantId = generateId();\n      assistantIdRef.current = assistantId;\n      const assistantMsg: ChatMessage = {\n        id: assistantId,\n        role: 'assistant',\n        content: '',\n        toolCalls: [],\n        isStreaming: true,\n      };\n\n      setMessages((prev) => [...prev, userMsg, assistantMsg]);\n      setIsStreaming(true);\n      textBufferRef.current = '';\n      segmentsRef.current = [];\n      currentTextRef.current = '';\n\n      const controller = new AbortController();\n      abortRef.current = controller;\n\n      await runAgentLoop(\n        text,\n        sessionIdRef.current,\n        {\n          onTextChunk: (chunk) => {\n            textBufferRef.current += chunk;\n            if (!flushTimerRef.current) {\n              flushTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {\n                flushText();\n                flushTimerRef.current = null;\n              }, 50);\n            }\n          },\n          onThinkingDone: () => {\n            // Extended thinking just ended and a text block is starting.\n            // Create a thinking segment from accumulated thinking text.\n            if (flushTimerRef.current) {\n              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);\n              flushTimerRef.current = null;\n            }\n            flushText();\n            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {\n              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'thinking', text: currentTextRef.current });\n              currentTextRef.current = '';\n            }\n            setMessages((prev) =>\n              prev.map((m) =>\n                m.id === assistantId\n                  ? { ...m, currentPhase: 'answer', segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }\n                  : m\n              )\n            );\n          },\n          onToolCall: (toolCallId: string, sql: string) => {\n            if (flushTimerRef.current) {\n              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);\n              flushTimerRef.current = null;\n            }\n            flushText();\n            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {\n              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'thinking', text: currentTextRef.current });\n              currentTextRef.current = '';\n            }\n            // Add a pending tool segment so the SQL is shown immediately\n            segmentsRef.current.push({\n              type: 'tool',\n              toolResult: { toolCallId, sql, columns: [], rows: [], rowCount: 0 },\n            });\n            setMessages((prev) =>\n              prev.map((m) =>\n                m.id === assistantId\n                  ? { ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }\n                  : m\n              )\n            );\n          },\n          onToolResult: (result: ToolCallResult) => {\n            // Replace the pending tool segment with the full result\n            const pendingIdx = segmentsRef.current.findIndex(\n              (s) => s.type === 'tool' && s.toolResult?.toolCallId === result.toolCallId\n            );\n            if (pendingIdx !== -1) {\n              segmentsRef.current[pendingIdx] = { type: 'tool', toolResult: result };\n            } else {\n              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'tool', toolResult: result });\n            }\n            setMessages((prev) =>\n              prev.map((m) =>\n                m.id === assistantId\n                  ? { ...m, toolCalls: [...(m.toolCalls || []), result], segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }\n                  : m\n              )\n            );\n            refreshTables();\n          },\n          onDone: (newSessionId) => {\n            if (newSessionId) sessionIdRef.current = newSessionId;\n            if (flushTimerRef.current) {\n              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);\n              flushTimerRef.current = null;\n            }\n            flushText();\n            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {\n              segmentsRef.current.push({\n                type: 'answer',\n                text: currentTextRef.current,\n              });\n              currentTextRef.current = '';\n            }\n            setMessages((prev) =>\n              prev.map((m) =>\n                m.id === assistantId\n                  ? { ...m, isStreaming: false, currentPhase: undefined, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }\n                  : m\n              )\n            );\n            setIsStreaming(false);\n            abortRef.current = null;\n          },\n          onError: (error) => {\n            if (flushTimerRef.current) {\n              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);\n              flushTimerRef.current = null;\n            }\n            flushText();\n            setMessages((prev) =>\n              prev.map((m) =>\n                m.id === assistantId\n                  ? { ...m, content: m.content + `\\n\\n**Error:** ${error}`, isStreaming: false }\n                  : m\n              )\n            );\n            setIsStreaming(false);\n            abortRef.current = null;\n          },\n        },\n        controller.signal\n      );\n    },\n    [isStreaming, messages, flushText, refreshTables]\n  );\n\n  const clearMessages = useCallback(() => {\n    if (abortRef.current) {\n      abortRef.current.abort();\n    }\n    setMessages([]);\n    setIsStreaming(false);\n    sessionIdRef.current = null;\n  }, []);\n\n  return (\n    <AgentContext.Provider\n      value={{ messages, isStreaming, sendMessage, clearMessages }}\n    >\n      {children}\n    </AgentContext.Provider>\n  );\n}\n\nexport function useAgent() {\n  return useContext(AgentContext);\n}\n"
}

Good. Now let me trace through the scenarios to verify correctness:

**Scenario A: Extended thinking + text answer (no tools)**
```
Backend sends: thinking → thinking → ... → thinking_done → thinking → thinking → ...→ done
                (thinking_delta)            (text starts)    (text_delta, no tool calls)
Frontend:       onTextChunk...              onThinkingDone   onTextChunk...              onDone
                                            → thinking seg                              → answer seg
```
Result: `[thinking segment] [answer segment]` -- Correct!

**Scenario B: Extended thinking + tool use + text answer**
```
Backend: thinking... → thinking_done → thinking... → tool_call → tool_result → thinking... → thinking_done → answer... → done
         (thinking_delta) (text starts) (text_delta)                          (thinking_delta) (text starts) (text_delta)
Frontend: onTextChunk   onThinkingDone  onTextChunk  onToolCall  onToolResult  onTextChunk   onThinkingDone onTextChunk  onDone
          (accumulate)  → thinking seg  (accumulate) → thinking  → update      (accumulate)  → thinking seg (accumulate) → answer seg
                                                       seg+tool
```
Result: `[thinking] [thinking: pre-tool text] [tool] [thinking: round 2] [answer]` -- Correct!

**Scenario C: No extended thinking, text only (no tools)**
```
Backend: thinking... → done   (text_delta only, has_thinking=false, no thinking_done emitted)
Frontend: onTextChunk  onDone → answer seg
```
Result: `[answer segment]` (no thinking block, just answer) -- Correct!

**Scenario D: No extended thinking, with tool calls**
```
Backend: thinking... → tool_call → tool_result → answer... → done
Frontend: onTextChunk  onToolCall  onToolResult  onTextChunk  onDone
                       → thinking+tool           (accumulate) → answer seg
```
Result: `[thinking: pre-tool] [tool] [answer]` -- Correct! (same as original behavior)

All scenarios check out. Please try it again — the thinking block should now show Claude's reasoning, and the answer text should appear below it.

.PHONY: dev backend frontend install install-backend install-frontend clean

# Run both backend and frontend concurrently
dev:
	@trap 'kill 0' EXIT; \
	cd backend && poetry run uvicorn app.main:app --reload --port 8000 & \
	cd frontend && npm run dev & \
	wait

backend:
	cd backend && poetry run uvicorn app.main:app --reload --port 8000

frontend:
	cd frontend && npm run dev

# Install all dependencies
install: install-backend install-frontend

install-backend:
	cd backend && poetry install

install-frontend:
	cd frontend && npm install

clean:
	rm -rf backend/.venv backend/__pycache__ backend/app/__pycache__ backend/app/routes/__pycache__
	rm -rf frontend/node_modules frontend/dist
```

> AGENT

Now I see the real issues:

1. **The agent calls `Bash` tools** (not `execute_sql`) for system info. Our code does `block.input.get("sql", "")` which returns `""` for Bash tools, creating empty tool segments (the horizontal lines).
2. **The answer DOES come through** as `answer` events after the tools, so `thinking_done` fix works. But the empty tool segments are the visual noise.

Let me fix `agent.py` to handle non-SQL tool calls properly:

> TOOL

```
     1→import json
     2→import logging
     3→from typing import AsyncIterator
     4→
     5→from claude_agent_sdk import (
     6→    ClaudeSDKClient,
     7→    ClaudeAgentOptions,
     8→    AssistantMessage,
     9→    ResultMessage,
    10→    ToolUseBlock,
    11→)
    12→from claude_agent_sdk._errors import MessageParseError
    13→from claude_agent_sdk._internal.message_parser import parse_message
    14→from claude_agent_sdk.types import StreamEvent
    15→from app.tools import create_duckdb_server
    16→from app.database import db
    17→
    18→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    19→
    20→
    21→def build_system_prompt() -> str:
    22→    tables = db.list_tables()
    23→    prompt = """You are a helpful data analyst assistant working with a DuckDB database.
    24→You can execute SQL queries using the execute_sql tool to answer questions about the user's data.
    25→
    26→Guidelines:
    27→- Write clear, efficient DuckDB SQL queries
    28→- When exploring data, start with small queries (use LIMIT)
    29→- Explain your findings in plain language after getting results
    30→- If a query fails, try to fix it and retry
    31→- Use double quotes for table and column names that might conflict with reserved words
    32→"""
    33→    if not tables:
    34→        prompt += "\nNo tables are currently loaded. Ask the user to upload a CSV file first."
    35→    else:
    36→        prompt += "\nCurrently loaded tables:\n"
    37→        for table in tables:
    38→            prompt += f'\nTable: "{table["name"]}" ({table["rowCount"]} rows)\nColumns:\n'
    39→            for col in table["columns"]:
    40→                prompt += f'  - "{col["name"]}" ({col["type"]})\n'
    41→    return prompt
    42→
    43→
    44→async def stream_chat(message: str, session_id: str | None = None) -> AsyncIterator[str]:
    45→    """Stream agent chat responses as SSE events."""
    46→    duckdb_server = create_duckdb_server()
    47→
    48→    options = ClaudeAgentOptions(
    49→        system_prompt=build_system_prompt(),
    50→        mcp_servers={"duckdb": duckdb_server},
    51→        allowed_tools=["mcp__duckdb__execute_sql"],
    52→        permission_mode="bypassPermissions",
    53→        max_turns=20,
    54→        include_partial_messages=True,
    55→    )
    56→
    57→    client = ClaudeSDKClient(options=options)
    58→    actual_session_id = session_id
    59→
    60→    try:
    61→        await client.connect()
    62→        await client.query(message, session_id=session_id or "default")
    63→
    64→        current_text = ""
    65→        has_tool_calls = False
    66→        thinking_sent = False
    67→        has_thinking = False
    68→
    69→        async for raw_data in client._query.receive_messages():
    70→            try:
    71→                msg = parse_message(raw_data)
    72→            except MessageParseError as e:
    73→                logger.debug("Skipping unrecognized message: %s", e)
    74→                continue
    75→
    76→            if isinstance(msg, StreamEvent):
    77→                event = msg.event
    78→                if not actual_session_id:
    79→                    actual_session_id = msg.session_id
    80→
    81→                event_type = event.get("type", "")
    82→
    83→                if event_type == "content_block_delta":
    84→                    delta = event.get("delta", {})
    85→                    delta_type = delta.get("type", "")
    86→                    if delta_type == "thinking_delta":
    87→                        text = delta.get("thinking", "")
    88→                        if text:
    89→                            current_text += text
    90→                            yield f"event: thinking\ndata: {json.dumps({'text': text})}\n\n"
    91→                    elif delta_type == "text_delta":
    92→                        text = delta.get("text", "")
    93→                        current_text += text
    94→                        event_name = "thinking" if not has_tool_calls else "answer"
    95→                        yield f"event: {event_name}\ndata: {json.dumps({'text': text})}\n\n"
    96→
    97→                elif event_type == "content_block_start":
    98→                    block = event.get("content_block", {})
    99→                    block_type = block.get("type")
   100→                    if block_type == "thinking":
   101→                        has_thinking = True
   102→                    elif block_type == "text":
   103→                        if has_thinking:
   104→                            yield f"event: thinking_done\ndata: {json.dumps({})}\n\n"
   105→                    elif block_type == "tool_use":
   106→                        if current_text.strip() and not thinking_sent:
   107→                            thinking_sent = True
   108→                        has_tool_calls = True
   109→
   110→            elif isinstance(msg, AssistantMessage):
   111→                if not actual_session_id:
   112→                    actual_session_id = "default"
   113→
   114→                for block in msg.content:
   115→                    if isinstance(block, ToolUseBlock):
   116→                        sql = block.input.get("sql", "")
   117→                        yield f"event: tool_call\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': block.id, 'sql': sql})}\n\n"
   118→                        has_tool_calls = True
   119→
   120→                        # Execute the query directly for the UI since
   121→                        # ToolResultBlock is not exposed in the SDK stream
   122→                        try:
   123→                            result = db.execute_query(sql)
   124→                            truncated = result["rows"][:100]
   125→                            yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': block.id, 'sql': sql, 'columns': result['columns'], 'rows': truncated, 'rowCount': result['rowCount']}, default=str)}\n\n"
   126→                        except Exception as e:
   127→                            yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': block.id, 'sql': sql, 'error': str(e)})}\n\n"
   128→
   129→            elif isinstance(msg, ResultMessage):
   130→                actual_session_id = msg.session_id
   131→                if msg.is_error and msg.result:
   132→                    yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': msg.result})}\n\n"
   133→                yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
   134→                break
   135→
   136→    except Exception as e:
   137→        yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': str(e)})}\n\n"
   138→    finally:
   139→        try:
   140→            await client.disconnect()
   141→        except Exception:
   142→            pass
   143→

```json
{
  "command": "CLAUDECODE= poetry run python -c \"\nimport asyncio, json\nfrom app.agent import stream_chat\n\nasync def test():\n    events = []\n    async for event in stream_chat('what is your os, cpu and memory info'):\n        for line in event.strip().split('\\n'):\n            if line.startswith('event:'):\n                event_type = line.split(': ', 1)[1]\n            elif line.startswith('data:'):\n                data = json.loads(line.split(': ', 1)[1])\n                text = data.get('text', '')\n                if event_type in ('thinking', 'answer'):\n                    # Summarize text chunks\n                    events.append(f'{event_type}: {text[:60]}...' if len(text) > 60 else f'{event_type}: {text}')\n                else:\n                    events.append(f'{event_type}: {json.dumps(data)[:100]}')\n        if 'event: done' in event or 'event: error' in event:\n            break\n    \n    # Print summary\n    print('=== EVENT SUMMARY ===')\n    thinking_count = sum(1 for e in events if e.startswith('thinking:'))\n    answer_count = sum(1 for e in events if e.startswith('answer:'))\n    tool_count = sum(1 for e in events if e.startswith('tool_call:'))\n    done_count = sum(1 for e in events if e.startswith('done:') or e.startswith('thinking_done:'))\n    print(f'thinking chunks: {thinking_count}')\n    print(f'thinking_done events: {sum(1 for e in events if e.startswith(\\\"thinking_done:\\\"))}')\n    print(f'tool_call events: {tool_count}')\n    print(f'tool_result events: {sum(1 for e in events if e.startswith(\\\"tool_result:\\\"))}')\n    print(f'answer chunks: {answer_count}')\n    print(f'done events: {sum(1 for e in events if e.startswith(\\\"done:\\\"))}')\n    print()\n    print('=== DISTINCT EVENT TYPES IN ORDER ===')\n    last_type = None\n    for e in events:\n        t = e.split(':')[0]\n        if t != last_type:\n            print(f'  -> {t}')\n            last_type = t\n\nasyncio.run(test())\n\" 2>&1",
  "description": "Test full flow and summarize events",
  "timeout": 120000
}
```

> AGENT

Now the flow is clean:
- `thinking` chunks (extended thinking)
- `thinking_done` (transition)
- `thinking` chunks (pre-tool text, sent as "thinking" since no `has_tool_calls` at stream level for non-SQL tools)
- `thinking_done` (another thinking→text transition after internal tool use)
- `answer` chunks (actual answer)
- `done`

thinking → thinking_done → thinking → thinking_done → answer → done

**SQL query** ("what tables do I have? show me the data"):

thinking → thinking_done → thinking → tool_call(SHOW TABLES) → tool_result → tool_call(SELECT *) → tool_result → thinking_done → answer → done

> AGENT

The fix is ready. Here's a summary of what was wrong and what changed:

## Root Cause

Two issues discovered by testing with the actual query:

1. **The agent uses `Bash` tools** (not `execute_sql`) for non-SQL questions. The code assumed all `ToolUseBlock`s had a `sql` field, so Bash calls produced empty tool segments (the horizontal lines).

2. **`has_thinking` flag mislabeled pre-tool `text_delta`** as "answer" events, which broke the frontend's segment creation flow and caused the actual answer text to disappear.

## Changes

**`backend/app/agent.py`:**
- `text_delta` labeling reverted to original logic: `"thinking" if not has_tool_calls else "answer"` (removed `has_thinking` from this check)
- Added `thinking_done` event emitted when a text block starts after an extended thinking block — this is the explicit signal for the frontend to create a thinking segment
- Non-SQL tool calls (where `sql` is empty, e.g. `Bash`) are now skipped for UI rendering — the SDK handles them internally

**`frontend/src/agent/agentService.ts`:**
- Replaced `onThinkingChunk`/`onAnswerChunk` with single `onTextChunk` + `onThinkingDone` callback

**`frontend/src/AgentContext.tsx`:**
- `onThinkingDone` handler creates a thinking segment from accumulated text and transitions to answer phase

> AGENT

The background server task completed (it was already stopped). The fix is deployed and ready for you to test — restart the app with `make dev` and try "what is your os, cpu and memory info" again. You should now see:

1. A **Thinking** block with Claude's reasoning
2. The **Answer** section with the actual system info (no more missing text or horizontal lines)

     1→import type { ToolCallResult } from '../types';
     2→
     3→interface AgentCallbacks {
     4→  onTextChunk: (text: string) => void;
     5→  onThinkingDone: () => void;
     6→  onToolCall: (toolCallId: string, sql: string) => void;
     7→  onToolResult: (result: ToolCallResult) => void;
     8→  onDone: (sessionId: string | null) => void;
     9→  onError: (error: string) => void;
    10→}
    11→
    12→export type { AgentCallbacks };
    13→
    14→export async function runAgentLoop(
    15→  message: string,
    16→  sessionId: string | null,
    17→  callbacks: AgentCallbacks,
    18→  signal?: AbortSignal,
    19→): Promise<void> {
    20→  try {
    21→    const response = await fetch('/api/chat', {
    22→      method: 'POST',
    23→      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    24→      body: JSON.stringify({ message, session_id: sessionId }),
    25→      signal,
    26→    });
    27→
    28→    if (!response.ok) {
    29→      const errorText = await response.text();
    30→      callbacks.onError(`Server error: ${errorText}`);
    31→      return;
    32→    }
    33→
    34→    const reader = response.body?.getReader();
    35→    if (!reader) {
    36→      callbacks.onError('No response stream');
    37→      return;
    38→    }
    39→
    40→    const decoder = new TextDecoder();
    41→    let buffer = '';
    42→
    43→    while (true) {
    44→      const { done, value } = await reader.read();
    45→      if (done) break;
    46→
    47→      buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
    48→      const lines = buffer.split('\n');
    49→      buffer = lines.pop() ?? '';
    50→
    51→      let eventType = '';
    52→      for (const line of lines) {
    53→        if (line.startsWith('event: ')) {
    54→          eventType = line.slice(7).trim();
    55→        } else if (line.startsWith('data: ')) {
    56→          const data = line.slice(6);
    57→          try {
    58→            const parsed = JSON.parse(data);
    59→            handleSSEEvent(eventType, parsed, callbacks);
    60→          } catch {
    61→            // Skip malformed JSON
    62→          }
    63→          eventType = '';
    64→        }
    65→      }
    66→    }
    67→  } catch (e: unknown) {
    68→    if (signal?.aborted) return;
    69→    const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Connection failed';
    70→    callbacks.onError(msg);
    71→  }
    72→}
    73→
    74→function handleSSEEvent(
    75→  eventType: string,
    76→  data: Record<string, unknown>,
    77→  callbacks: AgentCallbacks,
    78→) {
    79→  switch (eventType) {
    80→    case 'thinking':
    81→    case 'answer':
    82→      callbacks.onTextChunk(data.text as string);
    83→      break;
    84→    case 'thinking_done':
    85→      callbacks.onThinkingDone();
    86→      break;
    87→    case 'tool_call':
    88→      callbacks.onToolCall(data.id as string, data.sql as string);
    89→      break;
    90→    case 'tool_result': {
    91→      const result: ToolCallResult = {
    92→        toolCallId: (data.id as string) ?? '',
    93→        sql: (data.sql as string) ?? '',
    94→        columns: (data.columns as string[]) ?? [],
    95→        rows: (data.rows as Record<string, unknown>[]) ?? [],
    96→        rowCount: (data.rowCount as number) ?? 0,
    97→        error: (data.error as string) ?? undefined,
    98→        rawContent: (data.content as string) ?? undefined,
    99→      };
   100→      callbacks.onToolResult(result);
   101→      break;
   102→    }
   103→    case 'done':
   104→      callbacks.onDone((data.session_id as string) ?? null);
   105→      break;
   106→    case 'error':
   107→      callbacks.onError((data.message as string) ?? 'Unknown error');
   108→      break;
   109→  }
   110→}
   111→
```

     1→import json
     2→import logging
     3→from typing import AsyncIterator
     4→
     5→from claude_agent_sdk import (
     6→    ClaudeSDKClient,
     7→    ClaudeAgentOptions,
     8→    AssistantMessage,
     9→    ResultMessage,
    10→    ToolUseBlock,
    11→)
    12→from claude_agent_sdk._errors import MessageParseError
    13→from claude_agent_sdk._internal.message_parser import parse_message
    14→from claude_agent_sdk.types import StreamEvent
    15→from app.tools import create_duckdb_server
    16→from app.database import db
    17→
    18→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    19→
    20→
    21→def build_system_prompt() -> str:
    22→    tables = db.list_tables()
    23→    prompt = """You are a helpful data analyst assistant working with a DuckDB database.
    24→You can execute SQL queries using the execute_sql tool to answer questions about the user's data.
    25→
    26→Guidelines:
    27→- Write clear, efficient DuckDB SQL queries
    28→- When exploring data, start with small queries (use LIMIT)
    29→- Explain your findings in plain language after getting results
    30→- If a query fails, try to fix it and retry
    31→- Use double quotes for table and column names that might conflict with reserved words
    32→"""
    33→    if not tables:
    34→        prompt += "\nNo tables are currently loaded. Ask the user to upload a CSV file first."
    35→    else:
    36→        prompt += "\nCurrently loaded tables:\n"
    37→        for table in tables:
    38→            prompt += f'\nTable: "{table["name"]}" ({table["rowCount"]} rows)\nColumns:\n'
    39→            for col in table["columns"]:
    40→                prompt += f'  - "{col["name"]}" ({col["type"]})\n'
    41→    return prompt
    42→
    43→
    44→async def stream_chat(message: str, session_id: str | None = None) -> AsyncIterator[str]:
    45→    """Stream agent chat responses as SSE events."""
    46→    duckdb_server = create_duckdb_server()
    47→
    48→    options = ClaudeAgentOptions(
    49→        system_prompt=build_system_prompt(),
    50→        mcp_servers={"duckdb": duckdb_server},
    51→        allowed_tools=["mcp__duckdb__execute_sql"],
    52→        permission_mode="bypassPermissions",
    53→        max_turns=20,
    54→        include_partial_messages=True,
    55→    )
    56→
    57→    client = ClaudeSDKClient(options=options)
    58→    actual_session_id = session_id
    59→
    60→    try:
    61→        await client.connect()
    62→        await client.query(message, session_id=session_id or "default")
    63→
    64→        current_text = ""
    65→        has_tool_calls = False
    66→        thinking_sent = False
    67→        has_thinking = False
    68→
    69→        async for raw_data in client._query.receive_messages():
    70→            try:
    71→                msg = parse_message(raw_data)
    72→            except MessageParseError as e:
    73→                logger.debug("Skipping unrecognized message: %s", e)
    74→                continue
    75→
    76→            if isinstance(msg, StreamEvent):
    77→                event = msg.event
    78→                if not actual_session_id:
    79→                    actual_session_id = msg.session_id
    80→
    81→                event_type = event.get("type", "")
    82→
    83→                if event_type == "content_block_delta":
    84→                    delta = event.get("delta", {})
    85→                    delta_type = delta.get("type", "")
    86→                    if delta_type == "thinking_delta":
    87→                        text = delta.get("thinking", "")
    88→                        if text:
    89→                            current_text += text
    90→                            yield f"event: thinking\ndata: {json.dumps({'text': text})}\n\n"
    91→                    elif delta_type == "text_delta":
    92→                        text = delta.get("text", "")
    93→                        current_text += text
    94→                        event_name = "thinking" if not has_tool_calls else "answer"
    95→                        yield f"event: {event_name}\ndata: {json.dumps({'text': text})}\n\n"
    96→
    97→                elif event_type == "content_block_start":
    98→                    block = event.get("content_block", {})
    99→                    block_type = block.get("type")
   100→                    if block_type == "thinking":
   101→                        has_thinking = True
   102→                    elif block_type == "text":
   103→                        if has_thinking:
   104→                            yield f"event: thinking_done\ndata: {json.dumps({})}\n\n"
   105→                    elif block_type == "tool_use":
   106→                        if current_text.strip() and not thinking_sent:
   107→                            thinking_sent = True
   108→                        has_tool_calls = True
   109→
   110→            elif isinstance(msg, AssistantMessage):
   111→                if not actual_session_id:
   112→                    actual_session_id = "default"
   113→
   114→                for block in msg.content:
   115→                    if isinstance(block, ToolUseBlock):
   116→                        has_tool_calls = True
   117→                        sql = block.input.get("sql", "")
   118→                        if not sql:
   119→                            # Non-SQL tool call (e.g. Bash) — skip UI rendering,
   120→                            # the SDK handles execution internally
   121→                            continue
   122→
   123→                        yield f"event: tool_call\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': block.id, 'sql': sql})}\n\n"
   124→
   125→                        # Execute the query directly for the UI since
   126→                        # ToolResultBlock is not exposed in the SDK stream
   127→                        try:
   128→                            result = db.execute_query(sql)
   129→                            truncated = result["rows"][:100]
   130→                            yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': block.id, 'sql': sql, 'columns': result['columns'], 'rows': truncated, 'rowCount': result['rowCount']}, default=str)}\n\n"
   131→                        except Exception as e:
   132→                            yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': block.id, 'sql': sql, 'error': str(e)})}\n\n"
   133→
   134→            elif isinstance(msg, ResultMessage):
   135→                actual_session_id = msg.session_id
   136→                if msg.is_error and msg.result:
   137→                    yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': msg.result})}\n\n"
   138→                yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
   139→                break
   140→
   141→    except Exception as e:
   142→        yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': str(e)})}\n\n"
   143→    finally:
   144→        try:
   145→            await client.disconnect()
   146→        except Exception:
   147→            pass
   148→
```

Perfect! Now I have all the information I need. Let me create a comprehensive report:

## Complete Analysis of claude_agent_sdk Message Types

I've thoroughly explored the claude_agent_sdk package installed at `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/.venv/lib/python3.12/site-packages/claude_agent_sdk`. Here's the complete findings:

### ALL MESSAGE TYPES

The `parse_message()` function in `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/.venv/lib/python3.12/site-packages/claude_agent_sdk/_internal/message_parser.py` returns one of five message types (via the union type `Message`):

1. **UserMessage** - Messages from the user
2. **AssistantMessage** - Messages from Claude
3. **SystemMessage** - System notifications and metadata
4. **ResultMessage** - Final result/completion message
5. **StreamEvent** - Partial message updates during streaming

### CONTENT BLOCKS (what's inside messages)

Both `UserMessage` and `AssistantMessage` contain `content`, which is a list of content blocks. The available content block types are:

1. **TextBlock**
   - Fields: `text: str`

2. **ThinkingBlock**
   - Fields: `thinking: str`, `signature: str`

3. **ToolUseBlock** (Claude calls a tool)
   - Fields: 
     - `id: str` (unique identifier for this tool use)
     - `name: str` (the tool name)
     - `input: dict[str, Any]` (the input parameters passed to the tool)

4. **ToolResultBlock** (the output of a tool execution)
   - Fields:
     - `tool_use_id: str` (references the ToolUseBlock.id)
     - `content: str | list[dict[str, Any]] | None` (the tool's output)
     - `is_error: bool | None` (whether the tool call failed)

### DETAILED MESSAGE TYPE DEFINITIONS

**UserMessage** (in `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/.venv/lib/python3.12/site-packages/claude_agent_sdk/types.py`):
```python
@dataclass
class UserMessage:
    content: str | list[ContentBlock]
    uuid: str | None = None
    parent_tool_use_id: str | None = None
    tool_use_result: dict[str, Any] | None = None
```

**AssistantMessage**:
```python
@dataclass
class AssistantMessage:
    content: list[ContentBlock]
    model: str
    parent_tool_use_id: str | None = None
    error: AssistantMessageError | None = None
```

**SystemMessage**:
```python
@dataclass
class SystemMessage:
    subtype: str
    data: dict[str, Any]
```

**ResultMessage** (contains execution results):
```python
@dataclass
class ResultMessage:
    subtype: str
    duration_ms: int
    duration_api_ms: int
    is_error: bool
    num_turns: int
    session_id: str
    total_cost_usd: float | None = None
    usage: dict[str, Any] | None = None
    result: str | None = None
    structured_output: Any = None
```

**StreamEvent** (for partial streaming):
```python
@dataclass
class StreamEvent:
    uuid: str
    session_id: str
    event: dict[str, Any]  # The raw Anthropic API stream event
    parent_tool_use_id: str | None = None
```

### HOW TO ACCESS FROM parse_message() and receive_messages()

1. **parse_message()** - `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/.venv/lib/python3.12/site-packages/claude_agent_sdk/_internal/message_parser.py`
   - Input: `dict[str, Any]` (raw message data)
   - Output: `Message` (union of the 5 types above)
   - Raises: `MessageParseError` if parsing fails

2. **receive_messages()** - Available on `ClaudeSDKClient` via `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/.venv/lib/python3.12/site-packages/claude_agent_sdk/client.py`
   - Returns: `AsyncIterator[Message]`
   - Yields parsed messages as they arrive
   - Internally calls `parse_message()` on raw data

3. **query()** - `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/.venv/lib/python3.12/site-packages/claude_agent_sdk/query.py`
   - Returns: `AsyncIterator[Message]`
   - One-shot query interface that yields messages

### TOOL EXECUTION RESULTS ARE EXPOSED

YES - Tool execution results ARE exposed as message types:

1. **ToolUseBlock** appears in `AssistantMessage.content` when Claude wants to use a tool
2. **ToolResultBlock** appears in `AssistantMessage.content` or `UserMessage.content` containing the tool's output
3. Both blocks are linked by `tool_use_id` / `id` fields
4. `ResultMessage` provides overall execution metadata (cost, duration, usage, etc.)

### MESSAGE TYPE DISCRIMINATION

The message parser matches on the `type` field in the raw message:
- `"user"` → `UserMessage`
- `"assistant"` → `AssistantMessage`
- `"system"` → `SystemMessage`
- `"result"` → `ResultMessage`
- `"stream_event"` → `StreamEvent`

### EXPORTS

All message types are exported from the main `__init__.py` in the `__all__` list:

```python
__all__ = [
    # ... other exports ...
    "UserMessage",
    "AssistantMessage",
    "SystemMessage",
    "ResultMessage",
    "Message",
    "TextBlock",
    "ThinkingBlock",
    "ToolUseBlock",
    "ToolResultBlock",
    "ContentBlock",
    # ... other exports ...
]
```

     1→import json
     2→import logging
     3→from typing import AsyncIterator
     4→
     5→from claude_agent_sdk import (
     6→    ClaudeSDKClient,
     7→    ClaudeAgentOptions,
     8→    AssistantMessage,
     9→    ResultMessage,
    10→    ToolUseBlock,
    11→)
    12→from claude_agent_sdk._errors import MessageParseError
    13→from claude_agent_sdk._internal.message_parser import parse_message
    14→from claude_agent_sdk.types import StreamEvent
    15→from app.tools import create_duckdb_server
    16→from app.database import db
    17→
    18→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    19→
    20→
    21→def build_system_prompt() -> str:
    22→    tables = db.list_tables()
    23→    prompt = """You are a helpful data analyst assistant working with a DuckDB database.
    24→You can execute SQL queries using the execute_sql tool to answer questions about the user's data.
    25→
    26→Guidelines:
    27→- Write clear, efficient DuckDB SQL queries
    28→- When exploring data, start with small queries (use LIMIT)
    29→- Explain your findings in plain language after getting results
    30→- If a query fails, try to fix it and retry
    31→- Use double quotes for table and column names that might conflict with reserved words
    32→"""
    33→    if not tables:
    34→        prompt += "\nNo tables are currently loaded. Ask the user to upload a CSV file first."
    35→    else:
    36→        prompt += "\nCurrently loaded tables:\n"
    37→        for table in tables:
    38→            prompt += f'\nTable: "{table["name"]}" ({table["rowCount"]} rows)\nColumns:\n'
    39→            for col in table["columns"]:
    40→                prompt += f'  - "{col["name"]}" ({col["type"]})\n'
    41→    return prompt
    42→
    43→
    44→async def stream_chat(message: str, session_id: str | None = None) -> AsyncIterator[str]:
    45→    """Stream agent chat responses as SSE events."""
    46→    duckdb_server = create_duckdb_server()
    47→
    48→    options = ClaudeAgentOptions(
    49→        system_prompt=build_system_prompt(),
    50→        mcp_servers={"duckdb": duckdb_server},
    51→        allowed_tools=["mcp__duckdb__execute_sql"],
    52→        permission_mode="bypassPermissions",
    53→        max_turns=20,
    54→        include_partial_messages=True,
    55→    )
    56→
    57→    client = ClaudeSDKClient(options=options)
    58→    actual_session_id = session_id
    59→
    60→    try:
    61→        await client.connect()
    62→        await client.query(message, session_id=session_id or "default")
    63→
    64→        current_text = ""
    65→        has_tool_calls = False
    66→        thinking_sent = False
    67→        has_thinking = False
    68→
    69→        async for raw_data in client._query.receive_messages():
    70→            try:
    71→                msg = parse_message(raw_data)
    72→            except MessageParseError as e:
    73→                logger.debug("Skipping unrecognized message: %s", e)
    74→                continue
    75→
    76→            if isinstance(msg, StreamEvent):
    77→                event = msg.event
    78→                if not actual_session_id:
    79→                    actual_session_id = msg.session_id
    80→
    81→                event_type = event.get("type", "")
    82→
    83→                if event_type == "content_block_delta":
    84→                    delta = event.get("delta", {})
    85→                    delta_type = delta.get("type", "")
    86→                    if delta_type == "thinking_delta":
    87→                        text = delta.get("thinking", "")
    88→                        if text:
    89→                            current_text += text
    90→                            yield f"event: thinking\ndata: {json.dumps({'text': text})}\n\n"
    91→                    elif delta_type == "text_delta":
    92→                        text = delta.get("text", "")
    93→                        current_text += text
    94→                        event_name = "thinking" if not has_tool_calls else "answer"
    95→                        yield f"event: {event_name}\ndata: {json.dumps({'text': text})}\n\n"
    96→
    97→                elif event_type == "content_block_start":
    98→                    block = event.get("content_block", {})
    99→                    block_type = block.get("type")
   100→                    if block_type == "thinking":
   101→                        has_thinking = True
   102→                    elif block_type == "text":
   103→                        if has_thinking:
   104→                            yield f"event: thinking_done\ndata: {json.dumps({})}\n\n"
   105→                    elif block_type == "tool_use":
   106→                        if current_text.strip() and not thinking_sent:
   107→                            thinking_sent = True
   108→                        has_tool_calls = True
   109→
   110→            elif isinstance(msg, AssistantMessage):
   111→                if not actual_session_id:
   112→                    actual_session_id = "default"
   113→
   114→                for block in msg.content:
   115→                    if isinstance(block, ToolUseBlock):
   116→                        has_tool_calls = True
   117→                        sql = block.input.get("sql", "")
   118→                        command = block.input.get("command", "")
   119→
   120→                        if command:
   121→                            # Bash tool call — show command in thinking block
   122→                            tool_name = getattr(block, "name", "Bash") or "Bash"
   123→                            yield f"event: tool_call\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': block.id, 'tool': tool_name, 'command': command})}\n\n"
   124→                            continue
   125→
   126→                        if not sql:
   127→                            # Other non-SQL tool calls — skip UI rendering
   128→                            continue
   129→
   130→                        yield f"event: tool_call\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': block.id, 'sql': sql})}\n\n"
   131→
   132→                        # Execute the query directly for the UI since
   133→                        # ToolResultBlock is not exposed in the SDK stream
   134→                        try:
   135→                            result = db.execute_query(sql)
   136→                            truncated = result["rows"][:100]
   137→                            yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': block.id, 'sql': sql, 'columns': result['columns'], 'rows': truncated, 'rowCount': result['rowCount']}, default=str)}\n\n"
   138→                        except Exception as e:
   139→                            yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps({'id': block.id, 'sql': sql, 'error': str(e)})}\n\n"
   140→
   141→            elif isinstance(msg, ResultMessage):
   142→                actual_session_id = msg.session_id
   143→                if msg.is_error and msg.result:
   144→                    yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': msg.result})}\n\n"
   145→                yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
   146→                break
   147→
   148→    except Exception as e:
   149→        yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': str(e)})}\n\n"
   150→    finally:
   151→        try:
   152→            await client.disconnect()
   153→        except Exception:
   154→            pass
   155→
```

     1→import type { ToolCallResult } from '../types';
     2→
     3→interface AgentCallbacks {
     4→  onTextChunk: (text: string) => void;
     5→  onThinkingDone: () => void;
     6→  onToolCall: (toolCallId: string, sql: string, tool?: string, command?: string) => void;
     7→  onToolResult: (result: ToolCallResult) => void;
     8→  onDone: (sessionId: string | null) => void;
     9→  onError: (error: string) => void;
    10→}
    11→
    12→export type { AgentCallbacks };
    13→
    14→export async function runAgentLoop(
    15→  message: string,
    16→  sessionId: string | null,
    17→  callbacks: AgentCallbacks,
    18→  signal?: AbortSignal,
    19→): Promise<void> {
    20→  try {
    21→    const response = await fetch('/api/chat', {
    22→      method: 'POST',
    23→      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    24→      body: JSON.stringify({ message, session_id: sessionId }),
    25→      signal,
    26→    });
    27→
    28→    if (!response.ok) {
    29→      const errorText = await response.text();
    30→      callbacks.onError(`Server error: ${errorText}`);
    31→      return;
    32→    }
    33→
    34→    const reader = response.body?.getReader();
    35→    if (!reader) {
    36→      callbacks.onError('No response stream');
    37→      return;
    38→    }
    39→
    40→    const decoder = new TextDecoder();
    41→    let buffer = '';
    42→
    43→    while (true) {
    44→      const { done, value } = await reader.read();
    45→      if (done) break;
    46→
    47→      buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
    48→      const lines = buffer.split('\n');
    49→      buffer = lines.pop() ?? '';
    50→
    51→      let eventType = '';
    52→      for (const line of lines) {
    53→        if (line.startsWith('event: ')) {
    54→          eventType = line.slice(7).trim();
    55→        } else if (line.startsWith('data: ')) {
    56→          const data = line.slice(6);
    57→          try {
    58→            const parsed = JSON.parse(data);
    59→            handleSSEEvent(eventType, parsed, callbacks);
    60→          } catch {
    61→            // Skip malformed JSON
    62→          }
    63→          eventType = '';
    64→        }
    65→      }
    66→    }
    67→  } catch (e: unknown) {
    68→    if (signal?.aborted) return;
    69→    const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Connection failed';
    70→    callbacks.onError(msg);
    71→  }
    72→}
    73→
    74→function handleSSEEvent(
    75→  eventType: string,
    76→  data: Record<string, unknown>,
    77→  callbacks: AgentCallbacks,
    78→) {
    79→  switch (eventType) {
    80→    case 'thinking':
    81→    case 'answer':
    82→      callbacks.onTextChunk(data.text as string);
    83→      break;
    84→    case 'thinking_done':
    85→      callbacks.onThinkingDone();
    86→      break;
    87→    case 'tool_call':
    88→      callbacks.onToolCall(
    89→        data.id as string,
    90→        (data.sql as string) ?? '',
    91→        (data.tool as string) ?? undefined,
    92→        (data.command as string) ?? undefined,
    93→      );
    94→      break;
    95→    case 'tool_result': {
    96→      const result: ToolCallResult = {
    97→        toolCallId: (data.id as string) ?? '',
    98→        sql: (data.sql as string) ?? '',
    99→        columns: (data.columns as string[]) ?? [],
   100→        rows: (data.rows as Record<string, unknown>[]) ?? [],
   101→        rowCount: (data.rowCount as number) ?? 0,
   102→        error: (data.error as string) ?? undefined,
   103→        rawContent: (data.content as string) ?? undefined,
   104→      };
   105→      callbacks.onToolResult(result);
   106→      break;
   107→    }
   108→    case 'done':
   109→      callbacks.onDone((data.session_id as string) ?? null);
   110→      break;
   111→    case 'error':
   112→      callbacks.onError((data.message as string) ?? 'Unknown error');
   113→      break;
   114→  }
   115→}
   116→
```

     1→import {
     2→  createContext,
     3→  useCallback,
     4→  useContext,
     5→  useRef,
     6→  useState,
     7→  type ReactNode,
     8→} from 'react';
     9→import { runAgentLoop } from './agent/agentService';
    10→import type { ChatMessage, ContentSegment, TableInfo, ToolCallResult } from './types';
    11→
    12→interface AgentContextValue {
    13→  messages: ChatMessage[];
    14→  isStreaming: boolean;
    15→  sendMessage: (text: string) => void;
    16→  clearMessages: () => void;
    17→}
    18→
    19→const AgentContext = createContext<AgentContextValue>({
    20→  messages: [],
    21→  isStreaming: false,
    22→  sendMessage: () => {},
    23→  clearMessages: () => {},
    24→});
    25→
    26→function generateId() {
    27→  return Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10);
    28→}
    29→
    30→export function AgentProvider({
    31→  children,
    32→  refreshTables,
    33→}: {
    34→  children: ReactNode;
    35→  tables: TableInfo[];
    36→  refreshTables: () => Promise<void>;
    37→}) {
    38→  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<ChatMessage[]>([]);
    39→  const [isStreaming, setIsStreaming] = useState(false);
    40→  const abortRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);
    41→  const textBufferRef = useRef('');
    42→  const flushTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
    43→  const assistantIdRef = useRef('');
    44→  const segmentsRef = useRef<ContentSegment[]>([]);
    45→  const currentTextRef = useRef('');
    46→  const sessionIdRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
    47→
    48→  const flushText = useCallback(() => {
    49→    const text = textBufferRef.current;
    50→    if (!text) return;
    51→    const id = assistantIdRef.current;
    52→    currentTextRef.current += text;
    53→    setMessages((prev) =>
    54→      prev.map((m) =>
    55→        m.id === id ? { ...m, content: m.content + text } : m
    56→      )
    57→    );
    58→    textBufferRef.current = '';
    59→  }, []);
    60→
    61→  const sendMessage = useCallback(
    62→    async (text: string) => {
    63→      if (isStreaming) return;
    64→
    65→      const userMsg: ChatMessage = {
    66→        id: generateId(),
    67→        role: 'user',
    68→        content: text,
    69→      };
    70→
    71→      const assistantId = generateId();
    72→      assistantIdRef.current = assistantId;
    73→      const assistantMsg: ChatMessage = {
    74→        id: assistantId,
    75→        role: 'assistant',
    76→        content: '',
    77→        toolCalls: [],
    78→        isStreaming: true,
    79→      };
    80→
    81→      setMessages((prev) => [...prev, userMsg, assistantMsg]);
    82→      setIsStreaming(true);
    83→      textBufferRef.current = '';
    84→      segmentsRef.current = [];
    85→      currentTextRef.current = '';
    86→
    87→      const controller = new AbortController();
    88→      abortRef.current = controller;
    89→
    90→      await runAgentLoop(
    91→        text,
    92→        sessionIdRef.current,
    93→        {
    94→          onTextChunk: (chunk) => {
    95→            textBufferRef.current += chunk;
    96→            if (!flushTimerRef.current) {
    97→              flushTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
    98→                flushText();
    99→                flushTimerRef.current = null;
   100→              }, 50);
   101→            }
   102→          },
   103→          onThinkingDone: () => {
   104→            // Extended thinking just ended and a text block is starting.
   105→            // Create a thinking segment from accumulated thinking text.
   106→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   107→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   108→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   109→            }
   110→            flushText();
   111→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   112→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'thinking', text: currentTextRef.current });
   113→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   114→            }
   115→            setMessages((prev) =>
   116→              prev.map((m) =>
   117→                m.id === assistantId
   118→                  ? { ...m, currentPhase: 'answer', segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   119→                  : m
   120→              )
   121→            );
   122→          },
   123→          onToolCall: (toolCallId: string, sql: string, tool?: string, command?: string) => {
   124→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   125→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   126→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   127→            }
   128→            flushText();
   129→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   130→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'thinking', text: currentTextRef.current });
   131→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   132→            }
   133→            // Add a pending tool segment so the SQL/command is shown immediately
   134→            segmentsRef.current.push({
   135→              type: 'tool',
   136→              toolResult: { toolCallId, sql, columns: [], rows: [], rowCount: 0, tool, command },
   137→            });
   138→            setMessages((prev) =>
   139→              prev.map((m) =>
   140→                m.id === assistantId
   141→                  ? { ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   142→                  : m
   143→              )
   144→            );
   145→          },
   146→          onToolResult: (result: ToolCallResult) => {
   147→            // Replace the pending tool segment with the full result
   148→            const pendingIdx = segmentsRef.current.findIndex(
   149→              (s) => s.type === 'tool' && s.toolResult?.toolCallId === result.toolCallId
   150→            );
   151→            if (pendingIdx !== -1) {
   152→              segmentsRef.current[pendingIdx] = { type: 'tool', toolResult: result };
   153→            } else {
   154→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'tool', toolResult: result });
   155→            }
   156→            setMessages((prev) =>
   157→              prev.map((m) =>
   158→                m.id === assistantId
   159→                  ? { ...m, toolCalls: [...(m.toolCalls || []), result], segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   160→                  : m
   161→              )
   162→            );
   163→            refreshTables();
   164→          },
   165→          onDone: (newSessionId) => {
   166→            if (newSessionId) sessionIdRef.current = newSessionId;
   167→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   168→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   169→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   170→            }
   171→            flushText();
   172→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   173→              segmentsRef.current.push({
   174→                type: 'answer',
   175→                text: currentTextRef.current,
   176→              });
   177→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   178→            }
   179→            setMessages((prev) =>
   180→              prev.map((m) =>
   181→                m.id === assistantId
   182→                  ? { ...m, isStreaming: false, currentPhase: undefined, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   183→                  : m
   184→              )
   185→            );
   186→            setIsStreaming(false);
   187→            abortRef.current = null;
   188→          },
   189→          onError: (error) => {
   190→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   191→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   192→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   193→            }
   194→            flushText();
   195→            setMessages((prev) =>
   196→              prev.map((m) =>
   197→                m.id === assistantId
   198→                  ? { ...m, content: m.content + `\n\n**Error:** ${error}`, isStreaming: false }
   199→                  : m
   200→              )
   201→            );
   202→            setIsStreaming(false);
   203→            abortRef.current = null;
   204→          },
   205→        },
   206→        controller.signal
   207→      );
   208→    },
   209→    [isStreaming, messages, flushText, refreshTables]
   210→  );
   211→
   212→  const clearMessages = useCallback(() => {
   213→    if (abortRef.current) {
   214→      abortRef.current.abort();
   215→    }
   216→    setMessages([]);
   217→    setIsStreaming(false);
   218→    sessionIdRef.current = null;
   219→  }, []);
   220→
   221→  return (
   222→    <AgentContext.Provider
   223→      value={{ messages, isStreaming, sendMessage, clearMessages }}
   224→    >
   225→      {children}
   226→    </AgentContext.Provider>
   227→  );
   228→}
   229→
   230→export function useAgent() {
   231→  return useContext(AgentContext);
   232→}
   233→
```
