     1→import type { ToolCallResult } from '../types';
     2→import './InlineQueryResult.css';
     3→
     4→const MAX_DISPLAY_ROWS = 20;
     5→
     6→export function InlineQueryResult({ result }: { result: ToolCallResult }) {
     7→  // Bash command rendering
     8→  if (result.command) {
     9→    return (
    10→      <div className="inline-query inline-query--bash">
    11→        <div className="inline-query__label">{result.tool || 'Bash'}</div>
    12→        <div className="inline-query__sql">
    13→          <code>{result.command}</code>
    14→        </div>
    15→        {result.error && (
    16→          <div className="inline-query__error">{result.error}</div>
    17→        )}
    18→      </div>
    19→    );
    20→  }
    21→
    22→  if (result.error) {
    23→    return (
    24→      <div className="inline-query inline-query--error">
    25→        <div className="inline-query__sql">
    26→          <code>{result.sql}</code>
    27→        </div>
    28→        <div className="inline-query__error">{result.error}</div>
    29→      </div>
    30→    );
    31→  }
    32→
    33→  const displayRows = result.rows.slice(0, MAX_DISPLAY_ROWS);
    34→  const hasStructuredData = result.columns.length > 0;
    35→
    36→  return (
    37→    <div className="inline-query">
    38→      {result.sql && (
    39→        <div className="inline-query__sql">
    40→          <code>{result.sql}</code>
    41→        </div>
    42→      )}
    43→      {hasStructuredData ? (
    44→        <>
    45→          <div className="inline-query__table-wrapper">
    46→            <table className="inline-query__table">
    47→              <thead>
    48→                <tr>
    49→                  {result.columns.map((col) => (
    50→                    <th key={col}>{col}</th>
    51→                  ))}
    52→                </tr>
    53→              </thead>
    54→              <tbody>
    55→                {displayRows.map((row, i) => (
    56→                  <tr key={i}>
    57→                    {result.columns.map((col) => (
    58→                      <td key={col}>{String(row[col] ?? '')}</td>
    59→                    ))}
    60→                  </tr>
    61→                ))}
    62→              </tbody>
    63→            </table>
    64→          </div>
    65→          <div className="inline-query__meta">
    66→            {result.rowCount} row{result.rowCount !== 1 ? 's' : ''}
    67→            {result.rowCount > MAX_DISPLAY_ROWS && ` (showing ${MAX_DISPLAY_ROWS})`}
    68→          </div>
    69→        </>
    70→      ) : result.rawContent ? (
    71→        <div className="inline-query__raw">
    72→          <pre>{result.rawContent}</pre>
    73→        </div>
    74→      ) : result.sql ? (
    75→        <div className="inline-query__meta">Executing...</div>
    76→      ) : null}
    77→    </div>
    78→  );
    79→}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/frontend/src/agent/agentService.ts",
  "content": "import type { ToolCallResult } from '../types';\n\ninterface AgentCallbacks {\n  onTextChunk: (text: string) => void;\n  onThinkingDone: () => void;\n  onToolCall: (pending: ToolCallResult) => void;\n  onToolResult: (result: ToolCallResult) => void;\n  onDone: (sessionId: string | null) => void;\n  onError: (error: string) => void;\n}\n\nexport type { AgentCallbacks };\n\nexport async function runAgentLoop(\n  message: string,\n  sessionId: string | null,\n  callbacks: AgentCallbacks,\n  signal?: AbortSignal,\n): Promise<void> {\n  try {\n    const response = await fetch('/api/chat', {\n      method: 'POST',\n      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n      body: JSON.stringify({ message, session_id: sessionId }),\n      signal,\n    });\n\n    if (!response.ok) {\n      const errorText = await response.text();\n      callbacks.onError(`Server error: ${errorText}`);\n      return;\n    }\n\n    const reader = response.body?.getReader();\n    if (!reader) {\n      callbacks.onError('No response stream');\n      return;\n    }\n\n    const decoder = new TextDecoder();\n    let buffer = '';\n\n    while (true) {\n      const { done, value } = await reader.read();\n      if (done) break;\n\n      buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });\n      const lines = buffer.split('\\n');\n      buffer = lines.pop() ?? '';\n\n      let eventType = '';\n      for (const line of lines) {\n        if (line.startsWith('event: ')) {\n          eventType = line.slice(7).trim();\n        } else if (line.startsWith('data: ')) {\n          const data = line.slice(6);\n          try {\n            const parsed = JSON.parse(data);\n            handleSSEEvent(eventType, parsed, callbacks);\n          } catch {\n            // Skip malformed JSON\n          }\n          eventType = '';\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  } catch (e: unknown) {\n    if (signal?.aborted) return;\n    const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Connection failed';\n    callbacks.onError(msg);\n  }\n}\n\nfunction handleSSEEvent(\n  eventType: string,\n  data: Record<string, unknown>,\n  callbacks: AgentCallbacks,\n) {\n  switch (eventType) {\n    case 'thinking':\n    case 'answer':\n      callbacks.onTextChunk(data.text as string);\n      break;\n    case 'thinking_done':\n      callbacks.onThinkingDone();\n      break;\n    case 'tool_call':\n      callbacks.onToolCall({\n        toolCallId: (data.id as string) ?? '',\n        toolName: (data.name as string) ?? undefined,\n        sql: (data.sql as string) ?? '',\n        command: (data.command as string) ?? undefined,\n        toolInput: (data.input as Record<string, unknown>) ?? undefined,\n        columns: [],\n        rows: [],\n        rowCount: 0,\n      });\n      break;\n    case 'tool_result': {\n      const result: ToolCallResult = {\n        toolCallId: (data.id as string) ?? '',\n        toolName: (data.name as string) ?? undefined,\n        sql: (data.sql as string) ?? '',\n        columns: (data.columns as string[]) ?? [],\n        rows: (data.rows as Record<string, unknown>[]) ?? [],\n        rowCount: (data.rowCount as number) ?? 0,\n        error: (data.error as string) ?? undefined,\n        output: (data.output as string) ?? undefined,\n        rawContent: (data.content as string) ?? undefined,\n      };\n      callbacks.onToolResult(result);\n      break;\n    }\n    case 'done':\n      callbacks.onDone((data.session_id as string) ?? null);\n      break;\n    case 'error':\n      callbacks.onError((data.message as string) ?? 'Unknown error');\n      break;\n  }\n}\n"
}

  "content": "import type { ToolCallResult } from '../types';\nimport './InlineQueryResult.css';\n\nconst MAX_DISPLAY_ROWS = 20;\n\nfunction getToolDisplayName(result: ToolCallResult): string {\n  const name = result.toolName || '';\n  if (name.includes('execute_sql')) return 'SQL Query';\n  if (name === 'Bash') return 'Bash';\n  if (name === 'Read') return 'Read File';\n  if (name === 'Write') return 'Write File';\n  if (name === 'Edit') return 'Edit File';\n  if (name.startsWith('mcp__')) {\n    // e.g. \"mcp__server__tool_name\" -> \"tool_name\"\n    const parts = name.split('__');\n    return parts[parts.length - 1] || name;\n  }\n  return name || 'Tool';\n}\n\nfunction getToolInputDisplay(result: ToolCallResult): string | null {\n  if (result.sql) return result.sql;\n  if (result.command) return result.command;\n  if (result.toolInput) {\n    const entries = Object.entries(result.toolInput);\n    if (entries.length === 0) return null;\n    // For single-value inputs, show just the value\n    if (entries.length === 1) return String(entries[0][1]);\n    return JSON.stringify(result.toolInput, null, 2);\n  }\n  return null;\n}\n\nfunction isSQL(result: ToolCallResult): boolean {\n  return !!result.sql && (result.toolName?.includes('execute_sql') ?? false);\n}\n\nexport function InlineQueryResult({ result }: { result: ToolCallResult }) {\n  const displayName = getToolDisplayName(result);\n  const inputDisplay = getToolInputDisplay(result);\n  const isSQLTool = isSQL(result);\n  const hasStructuredData = result.columns.length > 0;\n  const displayRows = result.rows.slice(0, MAX_DISPLAY_ROWS);\n\n  // Determine border color class\n  let variantClass = '';\n  if (result.error) variantClass = 'inline-query--error';\n  else if (result.command) variantClass = 'inline-query--bash';\n  else if (!isSQLTool && !result.sql) variantClass = 'inline-query--generic';\n\n  return (\n    <div className={`inline-query ${variantClass}`}>\n      {/* Tool label */}\n      <div className={`inline-query__label ${isSQLTool ? 'inline-query__label--sql' : result.command ? 'inline-query__label--bash' : 'inline-query__label--generic'}`}>\n        {displayName}\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Tool input */}\n      {inputDisplay && (\n        <div className=\"inline-query__sql\">\n          <code>{inputDisplay}</code>\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {/* Error display */}\n      {result.error && (\n        <div className=\"inline-query__error\">{result.error}</div>\n      )}\n\n      {/* SQL structured results */}\n      {!result.error && isSQLTool && hasStructuredData && (\n        <>\n          <div className=\"inline-query__table-wrapper\">\n            <table className=\"inline-query__table\">\n              <thead>\n                <tr>\n                  {result.columns.map((col) => (\n                    <th key={col}>{col}</th>\n                  ))}\n                </tr>\n              </thead>\n              <tbody>\n                {displayRows.map((row, i) => (\n                  <tr key={i}>\n                    {result.columns.map((col) => (\n                      <td key={col}>{String(row[col] ?? '')}</td>\n                    ))}\n                  </tr>\n                ))}\n              </tbody>\n            </table>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"inline-query__meta\">\n            {result.rowCount} row{result.rowCount !== 1 ? 's' : ''}\n            {result.rowCount > MAX_DISPLAY_ROWS && ` (showing ${MAX_DISPLAY_ROWS})`}\n          </div>\n        </>\n      )}\n\n      {/* Text output for non-SQL tools */}\n      {!result.error && result.output && (\n        <div className=\"inline-query__output\">\n          <pre>{result.output}</pre>\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {/* Raw content fallback */}\n      {!result.error && !hasStructuredData && !result.output && result.rawContent && (\n        <div className=\"inline-query__raw\">\n          <pre>{result.rawContent}</pre>\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {/* Pending state */}\n      {!result.error && !hasStructuredData && !result.output && !result.rawContent && inputDisplay && (\n        <div className=\"inline-query__meta\">Executing...</div>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

> AGENT

Here's a summary of all changes:

### Backend (`agent.py`)
- **Imported** `UserMessage` and `ToolResultBlock` from the SDK
- **Generalized** `AssistantMessage` handler: emits `tool_call` SSE event for **ALL** tool types with `id`, `name`, and the relevant input (`sql`, `command`, or generic `input`)
- **Added** `UserMessage` handler: captures `ToolResultBlock` content and emits `tool_result` events with text `output` for non-SQL tools (Bash, Python, Read, etc.)
- **SQL tools** still get special handling with structured column/row results
- **Tracks** `sql_result_ids` to avoid duplicate results and `tool_names` to map IDs back to names

### Frontend (`types.ts`)
- Added `toolName?`, `toolInput?`, `output?` fields to `ToolCallResult`
- Removed the old `tool?` field in favor of `toolName`

### Frontend (`agentService.ts`)
- `onToolCall` now accepts a full `ToolCallResult` object (pending state) instead of individual parameters
- Parses `name`, `input`, `output` from SSE events

### Frontend (`AgentContext.tsx`)
- `onToolCall` passes the full pending `ToolCallResult` to segments
- `onToolResult` **merges** result data with pending segment info (preserves tool name, command, input from pending state)

### Frontend (`InlineQueryResult.tsx`)
- Completely redesigned to handle all tool types generically:
  - **SQL**: green label + SQL code + table results
  - **Bash**: purple label + command + text output
  - **Generic tools**: blue label + input display + text output
- Smart display name mapping (e.g., `mcp__duckdb__execute_sql` → "SQL Query")
- Shows "Executing..." while waiting for results

### Frontend (`InlineQueryResult.css`)
- Added color-coded label styles for SQL (green), Bash (purple), and generic (blue) tools
- Added `.inline-query__output` styles for text output display

     def execute_query(self, sql: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
         """Execute a SQL query and return results as dict with columns, rows, rowCount."""

+        if result is None:
+            return {"columns": [], "rows": [], "rowCount": 0}

         return {"columns": columns, "rows": rows, "rowCount": len(rows)}

+  if (name === 'Read') return 'Read File';
+  if (name === 'Write') return 'Write File';
+  if (name === 'Edit') return 'Edit File';
+  if (name.startsWith('mcp__')) {
+    // e.g. "mcp__server__tool_name" -> "tool_name"

+    return parts[parts.length - 1] || name;
